### [41] [StoryScope: Investigating idiosyncrasies in AI fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03136)
*Jenna Russell,Rishanth Rajendhran,Mohit Iyyer,John Wieting*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StoryScope通过分析话语层面的叙事特征（如角色能动性、时间复杂性等），在人类与AI生成故事检测中达到93.2%宏F1，六作者分类达68.4%，发现AI故事倾向于过度解释主题、单线情节，而人类故事更具道德模糊性和时间复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成小说日益普遍，作者身份和原创性问题变得至关重要。现有研究多关注表面写作风格特征，本文旨在探索是否可以不依赖风格信号，仅通过话语层面的叙事选择（如角色能动性、时间不连续性）来区分AI与人类创作的故事。

Method: 提出StoryScope管道，自动推导细粒度、可解释的话语层面叙事特征空间（10个维度）。应用于包含10,272个写作提示的平行语料库，每个提示由人类作者和五个LLM（共61,608个故事，每个约5,000词）创作，提取每个故事304个特征。使用30个核心叙事特征进行分析。

Result: 仅叙事特征在人类vs.AI检测中达到93.2%宏F1，六作者分类达68.4%，保留了包含风格线索模型97%以上的性能。AI故事倾向于过度解释主题、偏好整洁的单线情节，而人类故事将主角选择框架为更道德模糊且具有更高的时间复杂性。各模型有独特指纹特征：Claude事件升级平缓，GPT过度使用梦境序列，Gemini默认外部角色描述。AI生成故事在叙事空间中聚集，而人类创作故事表现出更大多样性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，底层叙事构建的差异（不仅仅是写作风格）可用于区分人类原创作品与AI生成小说。AI故事在叙事选择上表现出系统性模式，而人类创作展现出更大的叙事多样性，这为AI检测和作者身份识别提供了新视角。

Abstract: As AI-generated fiction becomes increasingly prevalent, questions of authorship and originality are becoming central to how written work is evaluated. While most existing work in this space focuses on identifying surface-level signatures of AI writing, we ask instead whether AI-generated stories can be distinguished from human ones without relying on stylistic signals, focusing on discourse-level narrative choices such as character agency and chronological discontinuity. We propose StoryScope, a pipeline that automatically induces a fine-grained, interpretable feature space of discourse-level narrative features across 10 dimensions. We apply StoryScope to a parallel corpus of 10,272 writing prompts, each written by a human author and five LLMs, yielding 61,608 stories, each ~5,000 words, and 304 extracted features per story. Narrative features alone achieve 93.2% macro-F1 for human vs. AI detection and 68.4% macro-F1 for six-way authorship attribution, retaining over 97% of the performance of models that include stylistic cues. A compact set of 30 core narrative features captures much of this signal: AI stories over-explain themes and favor tidy, single-track plots while human stories frame protagonist' choices as more morally ambiguous and have increased temporal complexity. Per-model fingerprint features enable six-way attribution: for example, Claude produces notably flat event escalation, GPT over-indexes on dream sequences, and Gemini defaults to external character description. We find that AI-generated stories cluster in a shared region of narrative space, while human-authored stories exhibit greater diversity. More broadly, these results suggest that differences in underlying narrative construction, not just writing style, can be used to separate human-written original works from AI-generated fiction.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Precision: Importance-Aware Recall for Factuality Evaluation in Long-Form LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03141)
*Nazanin Jafari,James Allan,Mohit Iyyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个同时评估长文本生成精确度和召回率的全面事实性评估框架，发现当前LLM在精确度上表现远好于召回率，事实不完整性是主要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事实性评估方法主要关注精确度（分解响应为原子声明并验证），但忽略了同样重要的召回率维度（是否包含应包含的相关事实）。需要全面评估长文本生成的事实性。

Method: 提出综合事实性评估框架，同时测量精确度和召回率。利用外部知识源构建参考事实并确定其是否在生成文本中被捕获。引入基于相关性和显著性的重要性感知加权方案。

Result: 当前LLM在精确度上的表现显著优于召回率，表明事实不完整性是长文本生成的主要限制。模型通常能更好地覆盖高度重要的事实，而非所有相关事实。

Conclusion: 需要同时考虑精确度和召回率的全面事实性评估框架，当前LLM在事实完整性方面存在显著不足，未来研究应关注提高模型的事实覆盖能力。

Abstract: Evaluating the factuality of long-form output generated by large language models (LLMs) remains challenging, particularly when responses are open-ended and contain many fine-grained factual statements. Existing evaluation methods primarily focus on precision: they decompose a response into atomic claims and verify each claim against external knowledge sources such as Wikipedia. However, this overlooks an equally important dimension of factuality: recall, whether the generated response covers the relevant facts that should be included. We propose a comprehensive factuality evaluation framework that jointly measures precision and recall. Our method leverages external knowledge sources to construct reference facts and determine whether they are captured in generated text. We further introduce an importance-aware weighting scheme based on relevance and salience. Our analysis reveals that current LLMs perform substantially better on precision than on recall, suggesting that factual incompleteness remains a major limitation of long-form generation and that models are generally better at covering highly important facts than the full set of relevant facts.

</details>


### [43] [Valence-Arousal Subspace in LLMs: Circular Emotion Geometry and Multi-Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03147)
*Lihao Sun,Lewen Yan,Xiaoya Lu,Andrew Lee,Jie Zhang,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 从LLM表示中识别情感维度子空间，建立情感控制向量，实现情感维度调节并影响模型拒绝和奉承行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型内部表示中是否存在与人类情感感知一致的情感维度结构，并研究如何通过情感维度控制来调节模型的行为表现

Method: 从21.1万条情感标注文本中提取情感控制向量，通过岭回归在模型自报告的效价-唤醒度分数上学习VA轴作为PCA主成分的线性组合，建立情感子空间

Result: 恢复的VA子空间呈现与人类情感感知一致的圆形几何结构；在4.4万词汇项上与人类众包VA评分相关；沿VA轴引导生成能单调调节输出的情感维度；唤醒度增加会降低拒绝行为、增加奉承行为

Conclusion: LLM表示中存在与人类情感感知一致的情感维度结构，通过情感维度控制可调节模型行为，拒绝相关标记位于低唤醒、负效价区域，VA引导直接调节其生成概率

Abstract: We present a method to identify a valence-arousal (VA) subspace within large language model representations. From 211k emotion-labeled texts, we derive emotion steering vectors, then learn VA axes as linear combinations of their top PCA components via ridge regression on the model's self-reported valence-arousal scores. The resulting VA subspace exhibits circular geometry consistent with established models of human emotion perception. Projections along our recovered VA subspace correlate with human-crowdsourced VA ratings across 44k lexical items. Furthermore, steering generation along these axes produces monotonic shifts in the corresponding affective dimensions of model outputs. Steering along these directions also induces near-monotonic bidirectional control over refusal and sycophancy: increasing arousal decreases refusal and increases sycophancy, and vice versa. These effects replicate across Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B, demonstrating cross-architecture generality. We provide a mechanistic account for these effects and prior emotionally-framed controls: refusal-associated tokens ("I can't," "sorry") occupy low-arousal, negative-valence regions, so VA steering directly modulates their emission probability.

</details>


### [44] [Beyond the Parameters: A Technical Survey of Contextual Enrichment in Large Language Models: From In-Context Prompting to Causal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03174)
*Prakhar Bansal,Shivangi Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM增强策略进行了系统性综述，提出了基于结构化上下文程度的统一分析框架，并提供了文献筛选、证据合成和部署决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型编码了丰富的世界知识，但仍受限于静态知识、有限上下文窗口和弱结构化因果推理。需要系统分析不同增强策略来解决这些限制。

Method: 提出了基于结构化上下文程度的统一分析框架，涵盖上下文学习、提示工程、RAG、GraphRAG和CausalRAG。建立了透明的文献筛选协议、声明审计框架和结构化跨论文证据合成方法。

Result: 提供了对不同增强策略的系统性比较分析，区分了高置信度发现和新兴结果。开发了部署导向的决策框架和可信检索增强NLP的具体研究优先级。

Conclusion: 该综述为LLM增强策略提供了统一的理论框架和实践指导，强调了结构化上下文在提升模型性能中的关键作用，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode vast world knowledge in their parameters, yet they remain fundamentally limited by static knowledge, finite context windows, and weakly structured causal reasoning. This survey provides a unified account of augmentation strategies along a single axis: the degree of structured context supplied at inference time. We cover in-context learning and prompt engineering, Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), GraphRAG, and CausalRAG. Beyond conceptual comparison, we provide a transparent literature-screening protocol, a claim-audit framework, and a structured cross-paper evidence synthesis that distinguishes higher-confidence findings from emerging results. The paper concludes with a deployment-oriented decision framework and concrete research priorities for trustworthy retrieval-augmented NLP.

</details>


### [45] [Reliability Gated Multi-Teacher Distillation for Low Resource Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03192)
*Dipto Sumit,Ankan Kumar Roy,Sadia Khair Rodela,Atia Haque Asha,Mourchona Afrin,Niloy Farhan,Farig Yousuf Sadeque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EWAD和CPDP方法用于多教师知识蒸馏，在低资源抽象摘要任务中通过教师间一致性路由监督，跨语言蒸馏在3.2倍压缩下保留71-122%的教师性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多教师知识蒸馏在低资源抽象摘要任务中的可靠性问题，探索如何通过教师间一致性来优化监督路由，解决复杂蒸馏方法可能导致的性能下降问题。

Method: 提出EWAD（熵加权一致性感知蒸馏）和CPDP（容量比例散度保持）两种方法。EWAD基于教师间一致性在教师蒸馏和黄金监督之间进行令牌级路由；CPDP对学生相对于异质教师的位置施加几何约束。

Result: 在孟加拉语数据集上，logit级KD提供最可靠的增益；复杂蒸馏改善短摘要的语义相似性但降低长输出质量；跨语言伪标签KD在3.2倍压缩下保留71-122%的教师ROUGE-L分数；多法官LLM评估揭示单法官流程中的校准偏差。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知蒸馏有助于表征多教师监督何时改善摘要性能，以及数据扩展何时优于损失工程优化。

Abstract: We study multiteacher knowledge distillation for low resource abstractive summarization from a reliability aware perspective. We introduce EWAD (Entropy Weighted Agreement Aware Distillation), a token level mechanism that routes supervision between teacher distillation and gold supervision based on inter teacher agreement, and CPDP (Capacity Proportional Divergence Preservation), a geometric constraint on the student position relative to heterogeneous teachers. Across two Bangla datasets, 13 BanglaT5 ablations, and eight Qwen2.5 experiments, we find that logit level KD provides the most reliable gains, while more complex distillation improves semantic similarity for short summaries but degrades longer outputs. Cross lingual pseudo label KD across ten languages retains 71-122 percent of teacher ROUGE L at 3.2x compression. A human validated multi judge LLM evaluation further reveals calibration bias in single judge pipelines. Overall, our results show that reliability aware distillation helps characterize when multi teacher supervision improves summarization and when data scaling outweighs loss engineering.

</details>


### [46] [Learning the Signature of Memorization in Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03199)
*David Ilić,Kostadin Cvejoski,David Stanojević,Evgeny Grigorenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出首个可迁移的基于学习的成员推理攻击LT-MIA，通过利用微调过程中构造的无限标记数据训练分类器，发现跨架构家族和数据域的记忆化不变特征，实现零样本迁移到未见过的模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型微调的成员推理攻击依赖于人工设计的启发式方法（如损失阈值、Min-K%、参考校准），这些方法受限于设计者的直觉。需要一种基于学习的方法来捕捉更复杂的记忆化模式，并实现跨架构的泛化能力。

Method: 提出LT-MIA方法：1）利用微调过程构造无限标记数据（成员状态已知）；2）在transformer模型上训练成员推理分类器；3）将成员推理重构为基于每token分布统计量的序列分类任务；4）通过训练多样性和规模实现泛化。

Result: 1）训练的分类器零样本迁移到未见过的Mamba、RWKV-4、RecurrentGemma架构，分别达到0.963、0.972、0.936 AUC；2）在transformer上达到0.908 AUC；3）在代码数据上达到0.865 AUC（仅用自然语言训练）；4）在transformer上比最强基线在0.1% FPR时TPR提高2.8倍。

Conclusion: 微调语言模型会产生跨架构家族和数据域可检测的记忆化不变特征，该特征仅源于梯度下降和交叉熵损失优化。基于学习的方法能有效捕捉这一信号，实现强大的跨架构零样本迁移，将成员推理带入深度学习时代。

Abstract: All prior membership inference attacks for fine-tuned language models use hand-crafted heuristics (e.g., loss thresholding, Min-K\%, reference calibration), each bounded by the designer's intuition. We introduce the first transferable learned attack, enabled by the observation that fine-tuning any model on any corpus yields unlimited labeled data, since membership is known by construction. This removes the shadow model bottleneck and brings membership inference into the deep learning era: learning what matters rather than designing it, with generalization through training diversity and scale. We discover that fine-tuning language models produces an invariant signature of memorization detectable across architectural families and data domains. We train a membership inference classifier exclusively on transformer-based models. It transfers zero-shot to Mamba (state-space), RWKV-4 (linear attention), and RecurrentGemma (gated recurrence), achieving 0.963, 0.972, and 0.936 AUC respectively. Each evaluation combines an architecture and dataset never seen during training, yet all three exceed performance on held-out transformers (0.908 AUC). These four families share no computational mechanisms, their only commonality is gradient descent on cross-entropy loss. Even simple likelihood-based methods exhibit strong transfer, confirming the signature exists independently of the detection method. Our method, Learned Transfer MIA (LT-MIA), captures this signal most effectively by reframing membership inference as sequence classification over per-token distributional statistics. On transformers, LT-MIA achieves 2.8$\times$ higher TPR at 0.1\% FPR than the strongest baseline. The method also transfers to code (0.865 AUC) despite training only on natural language texts. Code and trained classifier available at https://github.com/JetBrains-Research/learned-mia.

</details>


### [47] [BAS: A Decision-Theoretic Approach to Evaluating Large Language Model Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03216)
*Sean Wu,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Edward Phillips,Boyan Gao,Anshul Thakur,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了行为对齐分数（BAS），一种基于决策理论的度量标准，用于评估LLM置信度在包含弃权选项的决策场景中的可靠性。BAS通过显式的回答或弃权效用模型，聚合不同风险阈值下的实际效用，衡量决策层面的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经常在应该弃权的情况下产生自信但错误的答案。标准评估协议要求模型必须回答，且未考虑不同风险偏好下置信度应如何指导决策。需要一种能够评估LLM置信度在支持弃权感知决策方面表现的度量标准。

Method: 引入行为对齐分数（BAS），这是一种基于决策理论的度量标准。BAS源自显式的回答或弃权效用模型，通过聚合连续风险阈值下的实际效用，衡量决策层面的可靠性。该方法在理论上证明真实的置信度估计能最大化期望BAS效用，将校准与决策最优行为联系起来。BAS与对数损失等适当评分规则相关，但结构不同：对数损失对称惩罚欠自信和过度自信，而BAS施加不对称惩罚，强烈优先避免过度自信错误。

Result: 研究构建了跨多个LLM和任务的自我报告置信度可靠性基准。结果显示决策有用置信度存在显著差异，虽然更大更准确的模型倾向于获得更高的BAS，但即使是前沿模型仍然容易出现严重的过度自信。重要的是，具有相似ECE或AURC的模型可能表现出非常不同的BAS，突显了标准度量的局限性。简单干预（如top-k置信度获取和后处理校准）可以显著改善置信度可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估LLM置信度可靠性提供了原则性度量标准和全面基准。BAS填补了现有评估协议的空白，将置信度校准与决策质量直接联系起来，特别强调避免过度自信错误的重要性，为实际部署中的风险感知决策提供了更实用的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce confident but incorrect answers in settings where abstention would be safer. Standard evaluation protocols, however, require a response and do not account for how confidence should guide decisions under different risk preferences. To address this gap, we introduce the Behavioral Alignment Score (BAS), a decision-theoretic metric for evaluating how well LLM confidence supports abstention-aware decision making. BAS is derived from an explicit answer-or-abstain utility model and aggregates realized utility across a continuum of risk thresholds, yielding a measure of decision-level reliability that depends on both the magnitude and ordering of confidence. We show theoretically that truthful confidence estimates uniquely maximize expected BAS utility, linking calibration to decision-optimal behavior. BAS is related to proper scoring rules such as log loss, but differs structurally: log loss penalizes underconfidence and overconfidence symmetrically, whereas BAS imposes an asymmetric penalty that strongly prioritizes avoiding overconfident errors. Using BAS alongside widely used metrics such as ECE and AURC, we then construct a benchmark of self-reported confidence reliability across multiple LLMs and tasks. Our results reveal substantial variation in decision-useful confidence, and while larger and more accurate models tend to achieve higher BAS, even frontier models remain prone to severe overconfidence. Importantly, models with similar ECE or AURC can exhibit very different BAS due to highly overconfident errors, highlighting limitations of standard metrics. We further show that simple interventions, such as top-$k$ confidence elicitation and post-hoc calibration, can meaningfully improve confidence reliability. Overall, our work provides both a principled metric and a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating LLM confidence reliability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [48] [Xpertbench: Expert Level Tasks with Rubrics-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02368)
*Xue Liu,Xin Ma,Yuxin Ma,Yongchang Peng,Duo Wang,Zhoufutu Wen,Ge Zhang,Kaiyuan Zhang,Xinyu Chen,Tianci He,Jiani Hou,Liang Hu,Ziyun Huang,Yongzhe Hui,Jianpeng Jiao,Chennan Ju,Yingru Kong,Yiran Li,Mengyun Liu,Luyao Ma,Fei Ni,Yiqing Ni,Yueyan Qiu,Yanle Ren,Zilin Shi,Zaiyuan Wang,Wenjie Yue,Shiyu Zhang,Xinyi Zhang,Kaiwen Zhao,Zhenwei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XpertBench是一个用于评估大语言模型在真实专业领域能力的高保真基准，包含1,346个专家设计的任务，覆盖80个专业类别，采用ShotJudge评估范式，发现顶尖模型在专家级任务上存在显著性能瓶颈（最高成功率仅~66%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架存在领域覆盖狭窄、依赖通用任务或自评估偏差等问题，无法有效评估LLMs在复杂、开放式专家级认知任务上的真实能力，需要建立更贴近真实专业场景的评估基准。

Method: 构建XpertBench基准：1）收集1,346个由1,000多名领域专家（来自顶尖机构和行业实践者）提交的任务，覆盖金融、医疗、法律、教育及STEM/人文双轨研究等80个类别；2）每个任务采用详细评分标准，包含15-40个加权检查点；3）提出ShotJudge评估范式，使用经过专家少样本示例校准的LLM评估者来减轻自奖励偏差。

Result: 对最先进LLMs的评估显示：1）存在明显的性能天花板，即使领先模型最高成功率也仅为~66%，平均得分约55%；2）模型表现出领域特异性差异，在定量推理与语言合成方面存在非重叠优势；3）揭示了当前AI系统与专家级能力之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: XpertBench揭示了LLMs在专家级任务上的显著性能瓶颈，为从通用助手向专业协作伙伴的过渡提供了关键评估工具，强调了开发更专业化AI系统的重要性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit plateauing performance on conventional benchmarks, a pivotal challenge persists: evaluating their proficiency in complex, open-ended tasks characterizing genuine expert-level cognition. Existing frameworks suffer from narrow domain coverage, reliance on generalist tasks, or self-evaluation biases. To bridge this gap, we present XpertBench, a high-fidelity benchmark engineered to assess LLMs across authentic professional domains. XpertBench consists of 1,346 meticulously curated tasks across 80 categories, spanning finance, healthcare, legal services, education, and dual-track research (STEM and Humanities). These tasks are derived from over 1,000 submissions by domain experts--including researchers from elite institutions and practitioners with extensive clinical or industrial experience--ensuring superior ecological validity. Each task uses detailed rubrics with mostly 15-40 weighted checkpoints to assess professional rigor. To facilitate scalable yet human-aligned assessment, we introduce ShotJudge, a novel evaluation paradigm that employs LLM judges calibrated with expert few-shot exemplars to mitigate self-rewarding biases. Our empirical evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs reveals a pronounced performance ceiling: even leading models achieve a peak success rate of only ~66%, with a mean score around 55%. Models also exhibit domain-specific divergence, showing non-overlapping strengths in quantitative reasoning versus linguistic synthesis.. These findings underscore a significant "expert-gap" in current AI systems and establish XpertBench as a critical instrument for navigating the transition from general-purpose assistants to specialized professional collaborators.

</details>


### [49] [Compositional Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02434)
*Anugyan Das,Omkar Ghugarkar,Vishvesh Bhat,Asad Aali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种神经符号架构，用于解决抽象与推理语料库（ARC）任务，通过分离感知、神经引导的转换提议和符号一致性过滤，在无需任务特定微调的情况下提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯神经架构缺乏可靠组合泛化能力，而纯符号系统难以处理感知基础问题。因此需要结合两者优势的神经符号方法来解决ARC这类需要抽象推理的任务。

Method: 提出神经符号架构：从网格中提取对象级结构，使用神经先验从固定领域特定语言（DSL）中提出候选转换模式，通过跨示例一致性过滤假设。基于单元模式组合推理框架，为大型语言模型（LLMs）增强对象表示和转换提议能力。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2上，将基础LLM性能从16%提升到24.4%（公开评估集），与ARC Lang Solver结合后达到30.8%。证明该方法能改善泛化能力，减少对暴力搜索和基于采样的测试时扩展的依赖。

Conclusion: 分离感知、神经引导转换提议和符号一致性过滤的神经符号架构能够在不进行任务特定微调的情况下提升泛化能力，为抽象推理任务提供了有效的解决方案。已开源相关代码。

Abstract: We study structured abstraction-based reasoning for the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) and compare its generalization to test-time approaches. Purely neural architectures lack reliable combinatorial generalization, while strictly symbolic systems struggle with perceptual grounding. We therefore propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that extracts object-level structure from grids, uses neural priors to propose candidate transformations from a fixed domain-specific language (DSL) of atomic patterns, and filters hypotheses using cross-example consistency. Instantiated as a compositional reasoning framework based on unit patterns inspired by human visual abstraction, the system augments large language models (LLMs) with object representations and transformation proposals. On ARC-AGI-2, it improves base LLM performance from 16% to 24.4% on the public evaluation set, and to 30.8% when combined with ARC Lang Solver via a meta-classifier. These results demonstrate that separating perception, neural-guided transformation proposal, and symbolic consistency filtering improves generalization without task-specific finetuning or reinforcement learning, while reducing reliance on brute-force search and sampling-based test-time scaling. We open-source the ARC-AGI-2 Reasoner code (https://github.com/CoreThink-AI/arc-agi-2-reasoner).

</details>


### [50] [A Comprehensive Framework for Long-Term Resiliency Investment Planning under Extreme Weather Uncertainty for Electric Utilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02504)
*Emma Benjaminson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个四部分框架，用于电力公司在极端天气不确定性下的多目标资本规划优化，发现基于净现值排名的简单方法比复杂的基于模型的元启发式优化方法表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力公司面临需求爆炸性增长、资产老化和极端天气威胁，需要大规模资本投资。虽然已有严格的资本规划框架，但有机会扩展这些能力来解决不确定性下的多目标优化问题。

Method: 提出一个四部分框架：1) 将极端天气作为不确定性来源；2) 利用电网数字孪生；3) 使用蒙特卡洛模拟捕捉变异性；4) 应用多目标优化方法寻找最优投资组合。研究比较了电网感知优化方法与无模型方法。

Result: 研究发现，考虑到基于模型的元启发式优化方法的计算复杂性，更简单的净现值排名方法仅凭有限的电网知识就能找到更优的投资组合。

Conclusion: 在电力公司资本规划中，简单的净现值排名方法在极端天气不确定性下可能比复杂的基于模型的优化方法更有效，这挑战了传统认为需要复杂电网模型才能获得最优结果的假设。

Abstract: Electric utilities must make massive capital investments in the coming years to respond to explosive growth in demand, aging assets and rising threats from extreme weather. Utilities today already have rigorous frameworks for capital planning, and there are opportunities to extend this capability to solve multi-objective optimization problems in the face of uncertainty. This work presents a four-part framework that 1) incorporates extreme weather as a source of uncertainty, 2) leverages a digital twin of the grid, 3) uses Monte Carlo simulation to capture variability and 4) applies a multi-objective optimization method for finding the optimal investment portfolio. We use this framework to investigate whether grid-aware optimization methods outperform model-free approaches. We find that, in fact, given the computational complexity of model-based metaheuristic optimization methods, the simpler net present value ranking method was able to find more optimal portfolios with only limited knowledge of the grid.

</details>


### [51] [Interpretable Deep Reinforcement Learning for Element-level Bridge Life-cycle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02528)
*Seyyed Amirhossein Moayyedi,David Y. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于可解释强化学习的新方法，用于桥梁全生命周期优化管理，使用元素级状态表示，生成可理解的倾斜决策树策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年生效的新国家桥梁库存规范强调使用元素级状态进行基于风险的桥梁管理，但元素级状态表示（四维概率数组）扩展了状态空间，给制定最优全生命周期策略带来挑战

Method: 提出可解释强化学习方法：使用可微分软树模型作为行动者函数近似器，训练过程中采用温度退火过程，结合正则化和剪枝规则限制策略复杂度，生成确定性倾斜决策树形式的策略

Result: 该方法能够生成节点数量和深度合理的可解释全生命周期策略，可直接由人类理解和审计，并易于集成到现有桥梁管理系统中

Conclusion: 提出的可解释强化学习方法能够有效处理元素级桥梁状态表示，生成可理解且接近最优的全生命周期管理策略，在钢梁桥全生命周期优化问题中得到验证

Abstract: The new Specifications for the National Bridge Inventory (SNBI), in effect from 2022, emphasize the use of element-level condition states (CS) for risk-based bridge management. Instead of a general component rating, element-level condition data use an array of relative CS quantities (i.e., CS proportions) to represent the condition of a bridge. Although this greatly increases the granularity of bridge condition data, it introduces challenges to set up optimal life-cycle policies due to the expanded state space from one single categorical integer to four-dimensional probability arrays. This study proposes a new interpretable reinforcement learning (RL) approach to seek optimal life-cycle policies based on element-level state representations. Compared to existing RL methods, the proposed algorithm yields life-cycle policies in the form of oblique decision trees with reasonable amounts of nodes and depth, making them directly understandable and auditable by humans and easily implementable into current bridge management systems. To achieve near-optimal policies, the proposed approach introduces three major improvements to existing RL methods: (a) the use of differentiable soft tree models as actor function approximators, (b) a temperature annealing process during training, and (c) regularization paired with pruning rules to limit policy complexity. Collectively, these improvements can yield interpretable life-cycle policies in the form of deterministic oblique decision trees. The benefits and trade-offs from these techniques are demonstrated in both supervised and reinforcement learning settings. The resulting framework is illustrated in a life-cycle optimization problem for steel girder bridges.

</details>


### [52] [Competency Questions as Executable Plans: a Controlled RAG Architecture for Cultural Heritage Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02545)
*Naga Sowjanya Barla,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于知识图谱的神经符号架构，通过"规划-检索-生成"工作流程解决LLM在非物质文化遗产叙事生成中的事实准确性问题，将能力问题重新定义为可执行的叙事计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非物质文化遗产保护面临集体记忆随时间消退的挑战。虽然大语言模型能够生成引人入胜的叙事，但其存在事实不准确或"幻觉"问题，在需要真实性的文化遗产应用中不可靠。

Method: 提出基于知识图谱的神经符号架构，建立透明的"规划-检索-生成"工作流程。关键创新是将能力问题从设计时验证工具重新定义为运行时可执行的叙事计划，弥合高级用户角色与原子知识检索之间的差距，确保生成过程证据封闭且完全可审计。使用Live Aid KG多模态数据集验证，比较三种检索增强生成策略：纯符号KG-RAG、文本增强的Hybrid-RAG和结构感知的Graph-RAG。

Result: 实验揭示了符号检索的事实精确性、混合方法的上下文丰富性和基于图遍历的叙事连贯性之间的量化权衡。纯符号方法在事实准确性方面表现最佳，而图遍历方法在叙事连贯性方面更优，混合方法在两者之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计个性化和可控的叙事系统提供了可行的见解，通过神经符号方法解决了LLM在文化遗产应用中的事实准确性问题，将能力问题重新定义为可执行的叙事计划是一个关键创新。

Abstract: The preservation of intangible cultural heritage is a critical challenge as collective memory fades over time. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for generating engaging narratives, their propensity for factual inaccuracies or "hallucinations" makes them unreliable for heritage applications where veracity is a central requirement. To address this, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic architecture grounded in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) that establishes a transparent "plan-retrieve-generate" workflow for story generation. A key novelty of our approach is the repurposing of competency questions (CQs) - traditionally design-time validation artifacts - into run-time executable narrative plans. This approach bridges the gap between high-level user personas and atomic knowledge retrieval, ensuring that generation is evidence-closed and fully auditable. We validate this architecture using a new resource: the Live Aid KG, a multimodal dataset aligning 1985 concert data with the Music Meta Ontology and linking to external multimedia assets. We present a systematic comparative evaluation of three distinct Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) strategies over this graph: a purely symbolic KG-RAG, a text-enriched Hybrid-RAG, and a structure-aware Graph-RAG. Our experiments reveal a quantifiable trade-off between the factual precision of symbolic retrieval, the contextual richness of hybrid methods, and the narrative coherence of graph-based traversal. Our findings offer actionable insights for designing personalised and controllable storytelling systems.

</details>


### [53] [Mitigating LLM biases toward spurious social contexts using direct preference optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02585)
*Hyunji Nam,Dorottya Demszky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Debiasing-DPO方法，通过自监督训练减少LLMs对无关上下文信息的敏感性，在教育评估案例中显著降低偏见并提高预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在高风险决策中应用日益广泛，但对无关上下文信息的敏感性可能导致有害偏见。在教育评估等关键任务中，这种偏见会影响教师专业发展和职业轨迹。现有缓解方法（如提示工程和标准DPO）效果有限，需要更有效的去偏方法。

Method: 提出Debiasing-DPO自监督训练方法：1) 使用仅查询生成中性推理；2) 使用查询加额外无关上下文生成偏见推理；3) 将这两种推理配对进行训练。同时结合监督微调以保持预测准确性。在Llama 3B/8B和Qwen 3B/7B Instruct模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 无关上下文信息可使模型预测在7分量表上偏移达1.48分，更大模型有时表现出更高敏感性。Debiasing-DPO平均减少84%的偏见，提高52%的预测准确性。模型规模扩大并不自然带来对无关上下文的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对无关上下文的鲁棒性不是模型规模扩大的自然副产品。Debiasing-DPO方法在基于提示的预测任务中能显著提高准确性和鲁棒性，为LLMs在高风险决策中的可靠部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for high-stakes decision-making, yet their sensitivity to spurious contextual information can introduce harmful biases. This is a critical concern when models are deployed for tasks like evaluating teachers' instructional quality, where biased assessment can affect teachers' professional development and career trajectories. We investigate model robustness to spurious social contexts using the largest publicly available dataset of U.S. classroom transcripts (NCTE) paired with expert rubric scores. Evaluating seven frontier and open-weight models across seven categories of spurious contexts -- including teacher experience, education level, demographic identity, and sycophancy-inducing framings -- we find that irrelevant contextual information can shift model predictions by up to 1.48 points on a 7-point scale, with larger models sometimes exhibiting greater sensitivity despite higher predictive accuracy. Mitigations using prompts and standard direct preference optimization (DPO) prove largely insufficient. We propose **Debiasing-DPO**,, a self-supervised training method that pairs neutral reasoning generated from the query alone, with the model's biased reasoning generated with both the query and additional spurious context. We further combine this objective with supervised fine-tuning on ground-truth labels to prevent losses in predictive accuracy. Applied to Llama 3B \& 8B and Qwen 3B \& 7B Instruct models, Debiasing-DPO reduces bias by 84\% and improves predictive accuracy by 52\% on average. Our findings from the educational case study highlight that robustness to spurious context is not a natural byproduct of model scaling and that our proposed method can yield substantial gains in both accuracy and robustness for prompt-based prediction tasks.

</details>


### [54] [Do Audio-Visual Large Language Models Really See and Hear?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02605)
*Ramaneswaran Selvakumar,Kaousheik Jayakumar,S Sakshi,Sreyan Ghosh,Ruohan Gao,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次对音频-视觉大语言模型进行机制可解释性研究，发现尽管中间层编码了丰富的音频语义，但在音频与视觉冲突时，音频信息在最终文本生成中被视觉主导的融合层抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视觉大语言模型作为多模态感知的统一接口正在兴起，但对其内部工作机制的理解有限。本研究旨在通过机制可解释性分析，探索音频和视觉特征如何在AVLLM的不同层中演化、融合并最终产生文本输出。

Method: 采用机制可解释性研究方法，分析AVLLM中音频和视觉特征在不同层的演化过程。通过探测分析检查潜在音频信息的存在性，追踪融合层对不同模态的表征偏好，并分析训练过程对模态平衡的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1）AVLLM在中间层编码了丰富的音频语义；2）当音频与视觉冲突时，这些音频能力在最终文本生成中难以体现；3）有用的潜在音频信息确实存在，但更深层的融合层不成比例地偏向视觉表征，抑制音频线索；4）这种不平衡源于训练过程，AVLLM的音频行为与其视觉-语言基础模型高度匹配，表明对音频监督的额外对齐有限。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了AVLLMs中存在根本性的模态偏见，为理解多模态大语言模型如何整合音频和视觉提供了新的机制性见解。研究结果表明当前AVLLMs在模态融合中存在视觉主导的不平衡，这源于训练过程中对音频监督的有限对齐。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AVLLMs) are emerging as unified interfaces to multimodal perception. We present the first mechanistic interpretability study of AVLLMs, analyzing how audio and visual features evolve and fuse through different layers of an AVLLM to produce the final text outputs. We find that although AVLLMs encode rich audio semantics at intermediate layers, these capabilities largely fail to surface in the final text generation when audio conflicts with vision. Probing analyses show that useful latent audio information is present, but deeper fusion layers disproportionately privilege visual representations that tend to suppress audio cues. We further trace this imbalance to training: the AVLLM's audio behavior strongly matches its vision-language base model, indicating limited additional alignment to audio supervision. Our findings reveal a fundamental modality bias in AVLLMs and provide new mechanistic insights into how multimodal LLMs integrate audio and vision.

</details>


### [55] [AutoVerifier: An Agentic Automated Verification Framework Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02617)
*Yuntao Du,Minh Dinh,Kaiyuan Zhang,Ninghui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoVerifier是一个基于LLM的智能框架，用于自动化验证技术声明，通过六层结构化推理将技术文档转化为可追溯、有证据支持的情报评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学与技术情报分析需要验证复杂的技术声明，而现有方法无法弥合表面准确性与深层方法论有效性之间的验证差距，特别是在文献快速增长的情况下。

Method: AutoVerifier将每个技术断言分解为结构化声明三元组（主体、谓词、客体），构建知识图谱，通过六层渐进式丰富层进行结构化推理：语料库构建与摄取、实体与声明提取、文档内验证、跨源验证、外部信号佐证和最终假设矩阵生成。

Result: 在量子计算争议声明案例中，AutoVerifier自动识别了目标论文中的夸大声明和指标不一致性，追踪了跨源矛盾，发现了未披露的商业利益冲突，并生成了最终评估。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM验证能够可靠评估新兴技术的有效性和成熟度，将原始技术文档转化为可追溯、有证据支持的情报评估。

Abstract: Scientific and Technical Intelligence (S&TI) analysis requires verifying complex technical claims across rapidly growing literature, where existing approaches fail to bridge the verification gap between surface-level accuracy and deeper methodological validity. We present AutoVerifier, an LLM-based agentic framework that automates end-to-end verification of technical claims without requiring domain expertise. AutoVerifier decomposes every technical assertion into structured claim triples of the form (Subject, Predicate, Object), constructing knowledge graphs that enable structured reasoning across six progressively enriching layers: corpus construction and ingestion, entity and claim extraction, intra-document verification, cross-source verification, external signal corroboration, and final hypothesis matrix generation. We demonstrate AutoVerifier on a contested quantum computing claim, where the framework, operated by analysts with no quantum expertise, automatically identified overclaims and metric inconsistencies within the target paper, traced cross-source contradictions, uncovered undisclosed commercial conflicts of interest, and produced a final assessment. These results show that structured LLM verification can reliably evaluate the validity and maturity of emerging technologies, turning raw technical documents into traceable, evidence-backed intelligence assessments.

</details>


### [56] [OntoKG: Ontology-Oriented Knowledge Graph Construction with Intrinsic-Relational Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02618)
*Yitao Li,Zhanlin Liu,Anuranjan Pandey,Muni Srikanth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种本体导向的方法，通过内在-关系路由机制将知识图谱属性分类为内在或关系属性，生成可移植、可重用的声明式模式，应用于维基数据并验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将知识图谱结构决策嵌入流水线代码或临时提取关系，导致模式与构建过程紧密耦合，难以在下游本体级任务中重用。需要一种从一开始就为本体分析、实体消歧、领域定制和LLM引导提取而设计的模式。

Method: 采用本体导向方法，核心机制是内在-关系路由，将每个属性分类为内在属性或关系属性，并路由到相应的模式模块。在维基数据2026年1月转储上实例化该方法，包括基于规则的清理阶段和迭代路由过程。

Result: 从完整转储中识别出3460万实体的核心集，将属性分配到8个类别下的94个模块中。模式达到93.3%的类别覆盖率和98.0%的模块分配率。导出模式产生包含3400万节点和6120万边的属性图，涵盖38种关系类型。

Conclusion: 通过五个独立于构建流水线的应用验证了本体导向方法的有效性：本体结构分析、基准标注审计、实体消歧、领域定制和LLM引导提取。该方法产生的声明式模式可跨存储后端移植并独立重用。

Abstract: Organizing a large-scale knowledge graph into a typed property graph requires structural decisions -- which entities become nodes, which properties become edges, and what schema governs these choices. Existing approaches embed these decisions in pipeline code or extract relations ad hoc, producing schemas that are tightly coupled to their construction process and difficult to reuse for downstream ontology-level tasks. We present an ontology-oriented approach in which the schema is designed from the outset for ontology analysis, entity disambiguation, domain customization, and LLM-guided extraction -- not merely as a byproduct of graph building. The core mechanism is intrinsic-relational routing, which classifies every property as either intrinsic or relational and routes it to the corresponding schema module. This routing produces a declarative schema that is portable across storage backends and independently reusable.
  We instantiate the approach on the January 2026 Wikidata dump. A rule-based cleaning stage identifies a 34.6M-entity core set from the full dump, followed by iterative intrinsic-relational routing that assigns each property to one of 94 modules organized into 8 categories. With tool-augmented LLM support and human review, the schema reaches 93.3% category coverage and 98.0% module assignment among classified entities. Exporting this schema yields a property graph with 34.0M nodes and 61.2M edges across 38 relationship types. We validate the ontology-oriented claim through five applications that consume the schema independently of the construction pipeline: ontology structure analysis, benchmark annotation auditing, entity disambiguation, domain customization, and LLM-guided extraction.

</details>


### [57] [GrandCode: Achieving Grandmaster Level in Competitive Programming via Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02721)
*DeepReinforce Team,Xiaoya Li,Xiaofei Sun,Guoyin Wang,Songqiao Su,Chris Shum,Jiwei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrandCode是一个多智能体强化学习系统，首次在实时编程竞赛中击败所有人类选手，包括传奇大师级选手，标志着AI在最具竞争力的编程任务上超越最强人类程序员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在编程竞赛中仍落后于人类顶尖选手，最好的AI系统（如Google的Gemini~3 Deep Think）在非实时竞赛条件下仅获得第8名，无法与人类顶级选手竞争。这促使研究者开发能够真正超越人类选手的AI系统。

Method: 提出GrandCode多智能体RL系统，包含两个关键技术：1）协调多种智能体模块（假设提出、求解器、测试生成器、总结等），通过后训练和在线测试时RL联合改进；2）引入专门设计的Agentic GRPO，处理多阶段智能体展开中的延迟奖励和严重的离策略漂移问题。

Result: GrandCode在最近三场Codeforces实时竞赛（第1087轮、1088轮、1089轮）中均获得第一名，击败所有人类参与者，包括传奇大师级选手。这是首个在实时编程竞赛中持续击败所有人类选手的AI系统。

Conclusion: GrandCode证明AI系统已达到在最具竞争力的编程任务上超越最强人类程序员的水平，标志着编程竞赛这一人类最后堡垒已被AI攻破。

Abstract: Competitive programming remains one of the last few human strongholds in coding against AI. The best AI system to date still underperforms the best humans competitive programming: the most recent best result, Google's Gemini~3 Deep Think, attained 8th place even not being evaluated under live competition conditions. In this work, we introduce GrandCode, a multi-agent RL system designed for competitive programming. The capability of GrandCode is attributed to two key factors: (1) It orchestrates a variety of agentic modules (hypothesis proposal, solver, test generator, summarization, etc) and jointly improves them through post-training and online test-time RL; (2) We introduce Agentic GRPO specifically designed for multi-stage agent rollouts with delayed rewards and the severe off-policy drift that is prevalent in agentic RL. GrandCode is the first AI system that consistently beats all human participants in live contests of competitive programming: in the most recent three Codeforces live competitions, i.e., Round~1087 (Mar 21, 2026), Round~1088 (Mar 28, 2026), and Round~1089 (Mar 29, 2026), GrandCode placed first in all of them, beating all human participants, including legendary grandmasters. GrandCode shows that AI systems have reached a point where they surpass the strongest human programmers on the most competitive coding tasks.

</details>


### [58] [DeltaLogic: Minimal Premise Edits Reveal Belief-Revision Failures in Logical Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02733)
*Amit Dhanda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeltaLogic是一个基准测试转换协议，将自然语言推理示例转化为信念修正评估，揭示模型在证据微小变化下的信念修正能力不足


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理基准主要评估模型在固定前提下的正确推理能力，但忽视了动态环境中同样重要的信念修正能力——在证据发生最小变化时能否适当调整结论

Method: 提出DeltaLogic基准转换协议：将自然语言推理示例转化为修正片段，每个片段包含初始结论评估、最小编辑δ(P)应用、修正后结论评估三个步骤；基于FOLIO和ProofWriter实例化，使用受限标签评分评估小型因果语言模型

Result: 在30个片段的Qwen评估子集上，初始推理能力强并不保证信念修正能力强：Qwen3-1.7B初始准确率0.667但修正准确率仅0.467，在应改变结论的片段中惯性达0.600；Qwen3-4B保持相似失败模式；Phi-4-mini-instruct表现更强但仍有显著弃权和稳定性问题

Conclusion: 固定前提下的逻辑能力不意味着在局部证据编辑后能进行规范的信念修正，DeltaLogic针对这一独特且实用的推理能力，补充了现有逻辑推理和信念更新基准

Abstract: Reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate whether a model derives the correct answer from a fixed premise set, but they under-measure a closely related capability that matters in dynamic environments: belief revision under minimal evidence change. We introduce DeltaLogic, a benchmark transformation protocol that converts natural-language reasoning examples into short revision episodes. Each episode first asks for an initial conclusion under premises P, then applies a minimal edit δ(P), and finally asks whether the previous conclusion should remain stable or be revised. We instantiate DeltaLogic from FOLIO and ProofWriter and evaluate small causal language models with constrained label scoring. On a completed 30-episode Qwen evaluation subset, stronger initial reasoning still does not imply stronger revision behavior: Qwen3-1.7B reaches 0.667 initial accuracy but only 0.467 revision accuracy, with inertia rising to 0.600 on episodes where the gold label should change, while Qwen3-0.6B collapses into near universal abstention. There, Qwen3-4B preserves the same inertial failure pattern (0.650 initial, 0.450 revised, 0.600 inertia), whereas Phi-4-mini-instruct is substantially stronger (0.950 initial, 0.850 revised) but still exhibits non-trivial abstention and control instability. These results suggest that logical competence under fixed premises does not imply disciplined belief revision after local evidence edits. DeltaLogic therefore targets a distinct and practically important reasoning capability that complements existing logical inference and belief-updating benchmarks.

</details>


### [59] [Improving Role Consistency in Multi-Agent Collaboration via Quantitative Role Clarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02770)
*Guoling Zhou,Wenpei Han,Fengqin Yang,Li Wang,Yingcong Zhou,Zhiguo Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出量化角色清晰度方法，通过角色分配矩阵和角色清晰度矩阵来度量多智能体系统中角色一致性，并作为正则化器进行轻量微调，显著降低角色越界率并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM驱动的多智能体系统中，角色规范不服从（智能体未能遵守分配角色的职责和约束，可能表现得像其他角色）是一个主要故障模式。现有方法缺乏对角色一致性的量化度量，需要一种系统方法来改善角色一致性。

Method: 1. 构建角色分配矩阵S(φ)，其中s_{ij}(φ)表示第i个智能体行为轨迹与第j个智能体角色描述之间的语义相似度；2. 定义角色清晰度矩阵M(φ)=softmax(S(φ))-I，其中softmax(S(φ))是S(φ)的行向softmax，I是单位矩阵；3. 使用M(φ)的Frobenius范数量化角色描述与行为轨迹的对齐程度；4. 将角色清晰度矩阵作为正则化器在轻量微调过程中使用，以改善角色一致性。

Result: 在ChatDev多智能体系统上的实验表明：使用Qwen模型时，角色越界率从46.4%降至8.4%，角色清晰度得分从0.5328提升至0.9097，任务成功率从0.6769提升至0.6909；使用Llama模型时，角色越界率从43.4%降至0.2%，角色清晰度得分从0.5007提升至0.8530，任务成功率从0.6174提升至0.6763。

Conclusion: 提出的量化角色清晰度方法能有效改善多智能体系统中的角色一致性，显著降低角色越界率并提升任务性能，为LLM驱动的多智能体系统提供了一种有效的角色一致性优化框架。

Abstract: In large language model (LLM)-driven multi-agent systems, disobey role specification (failure to adhere to the defined responsibilities and constraints of an assigned role, potentially leading to an agent behaving like another) is a major failure mode \cite{DBLP:journals/corr/abs-2503-13657}. To address this issue, in the present paper, we propose a quantitative role clarity to improve role consistency. Firstly, we construct a role assignment matrix $S(φ)=[s_{ij}(φ)]$, where $s_{ij}(φ)$ is the semantic similarity between the $i$-th agent's behavior trajectory and the $j$-th agent's role description. Then we define role clarity matrix $M(φ)$ as $\text{softmax}(S(φ))-I$, where $\text{softmax}(S(φ))$ is a row-wise softmax of $S(φ)$ and $I$ is the identity matrix. The Frobenius norm of $M(φ)$ quantifies the alignment between agents' role descriptions and their behaviors trajectory. Moreover, we employ the role clarity matrix as a regularizer during lightweight fine-tuning to improve role consistency, thereby improving end-to-end task performance. Experiments on the ChatDev multi-agent system show that our method substantially improves role consistency and task performance: with Qwen and Llama, the role overstepping rate decreases from $46.4\%$ to $8.4\%$ and from $43.4\%$ to $0.2\%$, respectively, and the role clarity score increases from $0.5328$ to $0.9097$ and from $0.5007$ to $0.8530$, respectively, the task success rate increases from $0.6769$ to $0.6909$ and from $0.6174$ to $0.6763$, respectively.

</details>


### [60] [EMS: Multi-Agent Voting via Efficient Majority-then-Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02863)
*Yiqing Liu,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EMS：一种高效的多智能体投票方法，通过可靠性感知的智能体调度和早期停止机制，在达成多数共识时终止推理过程，减少32%的智能体调用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多数投票方法需要所有智能体完成推理才能聚合结果，导致大量计算开销，因为一旦达成多数共识，许多响应就变得冗余。需要提高多智能体投票的推理效率。

Method: 提出高效多数即停（EMS）框架，包含三个核心组件：1）智能体置信度建模（ACM）基于历史性能和语义相似度估计智能体可靠性；2）自适应增量投票（AIV）按可靠性顺序选择智能体并实现早期停止；3）个体置信度更新（ICU）动态更新贡献智能体的可靠性。

Result: 在六个基准测试上的广泛评估表明，EMS能够持续减少平均调用的智能体数量达32%。

Conclusion: EMS通过将多智能体投票重新定义为可靠性感知的智能体调度问题，实现了显著的效率提升，在达成多数共识时提前终止推理过程，减少了冗余计算。

Abstract: Majority voting is the standard for aggregating multi-agent responses into a final decision. However, traditional methods typically require all agents to complete their reasoning before aggregation begins, leading to significant computational overhead, as many responses become redundant once a majority consensus is achieved. In this work, we formulate the multi-agent voting as a reliability-aware agent scheduling problem, and propose an Efficient Majority-then-Stopping (EMS) to improve reasoning efficiency. EMS prioritizes agents based on task-aware reliability and terminates the reasoning pipeline the moment a majority is achieved from the following three critical components. Specifically, we introduce Agent Confidence Modeling (ACM) to estimate agent reliability using historical performance and semantic similarity, Adaptive Incremental Voting (AIV) to sequentially select agents with early stopping, and Individual Confidence Updating (ICU) to dynamically update the reliability of each contributing agent. Extensive evaluations across six benchmarks demonstrate that EMS consistently reduces the average number of invoked agents by 32%.

</details>


### [61] [Analysis of Optimality of Large Language Models on Planning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02910)
*Bernd Bohnet,Michael C. Mozer,Kevin Swersky,Wil Cunningham,Aaron Parisi,Kathleen Kenealy,Noah Fiedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在Blocksworld规划任务中表现出接近理论最优的性能，超越了传统规划器，即使在去除语义线索的抽象图任务中也能保持高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究前沿大语言模型在经典AI规划问题中的推理能力，特别是它们是否能够进行最优规划而非仅依赖启发式策略，并探索其背后的认知机制。

Method: 使用Blocksworld领域（带标签的积木塔移动任务）和形式等价的广义Path-Star图任务，系统操纵问题深度（塔高）、宽度（塔数）和组合性（目标积木数），比较LLMs与传统规划器（如LAMA）的性能。

Result: 推理增强的LLMs在复杂多目标配置中显著优于传统满足性规划器；即使搜索空间扩展，LLMs仍能接近理论最优性极限，在去除领域特定语义线索的抽象图任务中仍保持近乎完美的精度。

Conclusion: LLMs在规划任务中表现出超越传统搜索算法的性能，可能通过两种机制实现：通过推理令牌执行的算法模拟，以及允许模型将P*拓扑表示为可导航全局几何的几何记忆，从而有效规避指数组合复杂性。

Abstract: Classic AI planning problems have been revisited in the Large Language Model (LLM) era, with a focus of recent benchmarks on success rates rather than plan efficiency. We examine the degree to which frontier models reason optimally versus relying on simple, heuristic, and possibly inefficient strategies. We focus on the Blocksworld domain involving towers of labeled blocks which have to be moved from an initial to a goal configuration via a set of primitive actions. We also study a formally equivalent task, the generalized Path-Star ($P^*$) graph, in order to isolate true topological reasoning from semantic priors. We systematically manipulate problem depth (the height of block towers), width (the number of towers), and compositionality (the number of goal blocks). Reasoning-enhanced LLMs significantly outperform traditional satisficing planners (e.g., LAMA) in complex, multi-goal configurations. Although classical search algorithms hit a wall as the search space expands, LLMs track theoretical optimality limits with near-perfect precision, even when domain-specific semantic hints are stripped away. To explain these surprising findings, we consider (and find evidence to support) two hypotheses: an active Algorithmic Simulation executed via reasoning tokens and a Geometric Memory that allows models to represent the $P^*$ topology as a navigable global geometry, effectively bypassing exponential combinatorial complexity.

</details>


### [62] [FoE: Forest of Errors Makes the First Solution the Best in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02967)
*Kehan Jiang,Haonan Dong,Zhaolu Kang,Zhengzhou Zhu,Guojie Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现大型推理模型存在"第一个即最佳"现象，即后续推理路径不仅次优反而有害，挑战了测试时扩展定律。作者提出RED框架，通过"精炼首个"和"丢弃后续"策略，在提升性能的同时大幅减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型推理模型在复杂推理任务上表现出色，但研究发现它们存在一个反直觉现象：第一个推理路径往往是最优的，后续路径不仅次优反而可能有害。这挑战了测试时扩展定律，表明推理路径中的错误会随测试时间同步增长。作者旨在理解这一现象并开发更高效的推理框架。

Method: 1) 通过实证分析将错误表征为森林结构的"错误森林"；2) 提出RED自引导高效推理框架，包含两个组件：a) "精炼首个"：抑制首个解决方案中错误森林的增长；b) "丢弃后续"：通过双重一致性修剪后续错误森林。

Result: 在5个基准测试和6个骨干模型上的实验表明，RED优于8个竞争基线，性能提升最高达19.0%，同时减少37.7%~70.4%的token消耗。在错误森林指标上的比较实验揭示了RED的有效机制。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了大型推理模型中"第一个即最佳"现象及其背后的错误森林机制，挑战了测试时扩展定律。提出的RED框架通过抑制错误森林增长和修剪后续路径，实现了性能提升和计算效率的双重优化，为高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) like DeepSeek-R1 have demonstrated remarkable success in complex reasoning tasks, exhibiting human-like patterns in exploring multiple alternative solutions. Upon closer inspection, however, we uncover a surprising phenomenon: The First is The Best, where alternative solutions are not merely suboptimal but potentially detrimental. This observation challenges widely accepted test-time scaling laws, leading us to hypothesize that errors within the reasoning path scale concurrently with test time. Through comprehensive empirical analysis, we characterize errors as a forest-structured Forest of Errors (FoE) and conclude that FoE makes the First the Best, which is underpinned by rigorous theoretical analysis. Leveraging these insights, we propose RED, a self-guided efficient reasoning framework comprising two components: I) Refining First, which suppresses FoE growth in the first solution; and II) Discarding Subs, which prunes subsequent FoE via dual-consistency. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks and six backbone models demonstrate that RED outperforms eight competitive baselines, achieving performance gains of up to 19.0% while reducing token consumption by 37.7% ~ 70.4%. Moreover, comparative experiments on FoE metrics shed light on how RED achieves effectiveness.

</details>


### [63] [InfoSeeker: A Scalable Hierarchical Parallel Agent Framework for Web Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02971)
*Ka Yiu Lee,Yuxuan Huang,Zhiyuan He,Huichi Zhou,Weilin Luo,Kun Shao,Meng Fang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InfoSeeker框架，通过分层架构解决多源异构信息聚合中的上下文饱和、错误传播和高延迟问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能搜索系统过度关注深度多步推理，忽视了大规模信息合成中需要聚合大量异构证据的挑战，导致在数据密集型场景下面临上下文饱和、级联错误传播和高延迟等严重限制

Method: 基于近可分解性原则构建分层框架，包含战略Host、多个Manager和并行Worker三层架构，通过Manager层的聚合和反思机制实现严格上下文隔离，Worker层并行化加速任务执行

Result: 在两个互补基准测试中展示效率和效果优势：实现3-5倍速度提升，在WideSearch-en上达到8.4%成功率，在BrowseComp-zh上达到52.9%准确率

Conclusion: InfoSeeker框架通过分层设计和并行处理有效解决了大规模信息聚合中的关键挑战，在保持高效的同时提升了任务执行效果

Abstract: Recent agentic search systems have made substantial progress by emphasising deep, multi-step reasoning. However, this focus often overlooks the challenges of wide-scale information synthesis, where agents must aggregate large volumes of heterogeneous evidence across many sources. As a result, most existing large language model agent systems face severe limitations in data-intensive settings, including context saturation, cascading error propagation, and high end-to-end latency. To address these challenges, we present \framework, a hierarchical framework based on principle of near-decomposability, containing a strategic \textit{Host}, multiple \textit{Managers} and parallel \textit{Workers}. By leveraging aggregation and reflection mechanisms at the Manager layer, our framework enforces strict context isolation to prevent saturation and error propagation. Simultaneously, the parallelism in worker layer accelerates the speed of overall task execution, mitigating the significant latency. Our evaluation on two complementary benchmarks demonstrates both efficiency ($ 3-5 \times$ speed-up) and effectiveness, achieving a $8.4\%$ success rate on WideSearch-en and $52.9\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-zh. The code is released at https://github.com/agent-on-the-fly/InfoSeeker

</details>


### [64] [Automatic Textbook Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03071)
*Fabian Gloeckle,Ahmad Rammal,Charles Arnal,Remi Munos,Vivien Cabannes,Gabriel Synnaeve,Amaury Hayat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI系统在一周内将500多页研究生级代数组合学教材自动形式化为Lean代码，包含13万行代码和5900个声明，创造了教科书形式化规模的新里程碑


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动教科书自动形式化的规模和能力边界，从本科拓扑学和现有库内容重构扩展到完整的研究生级教材形式化，探索AI在数学形式化验证中的潜力

Method: 使用3万个Claude 4.5 Opus智能体通过版本控制并行协作，在共享代码库上工作一周，实现大规模多智能体软件工程

Result: 成功将500多页研究生代数组合学教材形式化为13万行Lean代码和5900个声明，形式化成本与人类专家团队薪资相当或更低，代码和蓝图网站已开源

Conclusion: 该研究展示了AI系统在数学教材大规模自动形式化方面的突破性能力，为数学形式化验证开辟了新途径，且仍有进一步优化效率的空间

Abstract: We present a case study where an automatic AI system formalizes a textbook with more than 500 pages of graduate-level algebraic combinatorics to Lean. The resulting formalization represents a new milestone in textbook formalization scale and proficiency, moving from early results in undergraduate topology and restructuring of existing library content to a full standalone formalization of a graduate textbook. The formalization comprises 130K lines of code and 5900 Lean declarations and was conducted within one week by a total of 30K Claude 4.5 Opus agents collaborating in parallel on a shared code base via version control, simultaneously setting a record in multi-agent software engineering with usable results. The inference cost matches or undercuts what we estimate as the salaries required for a team of human experts, and we expect there is still the potential for large efficiencies to be made without the need for better models. We make our code, the resulting Lean code base and a side-by-side blueprint website available open-source.

</details>


### [65] [Chart-RL: Policy Optimization Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced Visual Reasoning in Chart Question Answering with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03157)
*Yunfei Bai,Amit Dhanda,Shekhar Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chart-RL：基于强化学习的视觉语言模型框架，通过反馈驱动的策略优化提升图表问答能力，在减少参数和推理延迟的同时实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在图表问答任务中存在数值提取不精确、难以理解隐含视觉关系、空间关系注意力机制不足等局限性，需要更强大的推理能力来整合语言推理与视觉理解。

Method: 提出Chart-RL强化学习框架，通过策略优化技术和自适应奖励函数优化视觉感知与逻辑推理；集成LoRA参数高效微调，仅需单GPU配置；使用ChartQAPro数据集进行广泛基准测试。

Result: RL微调的Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct模型达到0.634的答案准确率，超越0.580的Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct基础模型（参数减半），同时推理延迟从31秒降至9秒。

Conclusion: Chart-RL框架有效解决了VLMs在图表问答中的关键挑战，通过强化学习优化实现了参数效率和推理速度的显著提升，同时保持或超越更大模型的性能。

Abstract: The recent advancements in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated progress toward true intelligence requiring robust reasoning capabilities. Beyond pattern recognition, linguistic reasoning must integrate with visual comprehension, particularly for Chart Question Answering (CQA) tasks involving complex data visualizations. Current VLMs face significant limitations in CQA, including imprecise numerical extraction, difficulty interpreting implicit visual relationships, and inadequate attention mechanisms for capturing spatial relationships in charts. In this work, we address these challenges by presenting Chart-RL, a novel reinforcement learning framework that enhances VLMs chart understanding through feedback-driven policy optimization of visual perception and logical inference. Our key innovation includes a comprehensive framework integrating Reinforcement Learning (RL) from Policy Optimization techniques along with adaptive reward functions, that demonstrates superior performance compared to baseline foundation models and competitive results against larger state-of-the-art architectures. We also integrated Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning through Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) in the RL framework that only requires single GPU configurations while preserving performance integrity. We conducted extensive benchmarking across open-source, proprietary, and state-of-the-art closed-source models utilizing the ChartQAPro dataset. The RL fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct model achieved an answer accuracy of 0.634, surpassing the 0.580 accuracy of the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct foundation model despite utilizing half the parameter count, while simultaneously reducing inference latency from 31 seconds to 9 seconds.

</details>


### [66] [Coupled Control, Structured Memory, and Verifiable Action in Agentic AI (SCRAT -- Stochastic Control with Retrieval and Auditable Trajectories): A Comparative Perspective from Squirrel Locomotion and Scatter-Hoarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03201)
*Maximiliano Armesto,Christophe Kolb*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出松鼠生态学作为研究智能体AI在部分可观测性、延迟和策略性观察下行动、记忆和验证能力的比较案例，建立了包含控制、记忆和验证的层次化模型，并提出三个可验证假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究往往将控制、记忆和验证分开研究，缺乏对这些能力耦合关系的系统性理解。作者认为松鼠生态学提供了一个理想的比较案例，因为松鼠的树栖运动、分散储藏和观众敏感型储藏行为同时耦合了这三种需求。

Method: 采用比较生物学方法，综合狐狸松鼠、东部灰松鼠和红松鼠的证据，建立明确的推理阶梯：经验观察→最小计算推断→AI设计猜想。引入一个最小层次化部分可观测控制模型，包含潜在动态、结构化情景记忆、观察者信念状态、选项级动作和延迟验证信号。

Result: 提出三个可验证假设：H1-快速局部反馈加预测补偿提高隐藏动态变化下的鲁棒性；H2-为未来控制组织的记忆改善线索冲突和负载下的延迟检索；H3-动作-记忆循环内的验证器和观察者模型减少静默故障和信息泄漏，但仍易受错误设定影响。下游猜想：角色分化的提议者/执行者/检查者/对抗者系统可减少不对称信息和验证负担下的相关错误。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一个比较视角和基准议程：一个关于控制、记忆和可验证动作耦合关系的可证伪主张的严谨研究计划，为智能体AI研究提供了新的方法论框架和验证基准。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly judged not by fluent output alone but by whether it can act, remember, and verify under partial observability, delay, and strategic observation. Existing research often studies these demands separately: robotics emphasizes control, retrieval systems emphasize memory, and alignment or assurance work emphasizes checking and oversight. This article argues that squirrel ecology offers a sharp comparative case because arboreal locomotion, scatter-hoarding, and audience-sensitive caching couple all three demands in one organism. We synthesize evidence from fox, eastern gray, and, in one field comparison, red squirrels, and impose an explicit inference ladder: empirical observation, minimal computational inference, and AI design conjecture. We introduce a minimal hierarchical partially observed control model with latent dynamics, structured episodic memory, observer-belief state, option-level actions, and delayed verifier signals. This motivates three hypotheses: (H1) fast local feedback plus predictive compensation improves robustness under hidden dynamics shifts; (H2) memory organized for future control improves delayed retrieval under cue conflict and load; and (H3) verifiers and observer models inside the action-memory loop reduce silent failure and information leakage while remaining vulnerable to misspecification. A downstream conjecture is that role-differentiated proposer/executor/checker/adversary systems may reduce correlated error under asymmetric information and verification burden. The contribution is a comparative perspective and benchmark agenda: a disciplined program of falsifiable claims about the coupling of control, memory, and verifiable action.

</details>

### [67] [Scaled Relative Graphs in Normed Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02407)
*Alberto Padoan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将SRG框架从希尔伯特空间扩展到赋范空间，引入正则配对和方向角，建立方向SRG理论用于算子性质验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ryu、Hannah和Yin提出的SRG框架在希尔伯特空间中有效，但需要扩展到更一般的赋范空间以处理更广泛的算子分析问题

Method: 用正则配对替代内积，引入方向角概念，构建方向SRG理论，推导SRG在缩放、求逆、加法和复合下的演算规则

Result: 方向SRG提供几何包含测试，可验证算子的收缩性和单调性等关键性质，理论通过数值示例验证，包括Bellman算子的图形收缩证明

Conclusion: 成功将SRG框架扩展到赋范空间，方向SRG为算子性质分析提供了有效的几何工具，在优化和强化学习等领域有应用潜力

Abstract: The paper extends the Scaled Relative Graph (SRG) framework of Ryu, Hannah, and Yin from Hilbert spaces to normed spaces. Our extension replaces the inner product with a regular pairing, whose asymmetry gives rise to directional angles and, in turn, directional SRGs. Directional SRGs are shown to provide geometric containment tests certifying key operator properties, including contraction and monotonicity. Calculus rules for SRGs under scaling, inversion, addition, and composition are also derived. The theory is illustrated by numerical examples, including a graphical contraction certificate for Bellman operators.

</details>


### [68] [On vehicle routing problems with stochastic demands -- Scenario-optimal recourse policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02496)
*Matheus J. Ota,Ricardo Fukasawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于场景追索不等式(SRIs)的新框架，用于解决具有随机需求的车辆路径问题(VRPSD)，相比现有方法显著提升了求解性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于整数L形(ILS)割平面，其系数针对特定追索策略定制，缺乏通用性。需要一种更通用的框架来处理VRPSD中的追索策略

Method: 将追索策略建模为高维混合整数规划的解，通过场景追索不等式(SRIs)在原始低维空间中刻画其凸包。SRIs适用于满足温和假设的任何追索策略

Result: 在场景最优追索策略下，SRIs支配多个ILS割平面。计算实验显示，使用SRIs的算法比最先进的ILS算法多求解329个实例到最优

Conclusion: 提出的SRIs框架为VRPSD提供了一种通用且强大的求解方法，显著优于现有的ILS方法，特别适用于场景最优追索策略

Abstract: Two-Stage Vehicle Routing Problems with Stochastic Demands (VRPSDs) form a class of stochastic combinatorial optimization problems where routes are planned in advance, demands are revealed upon vehicle arrival, and recourse actions are triggered whenever capacity is exceeded. Following recent works, we consider VRPSDs where demands are given by an empirical probability distribution of scenarios. Existing approaches rely on integer L-shaped (ILS) cuts, whose coefficients are tailored for specific recourse policies. In contrast, we propose a framework that casts recourse policies as solutions of a higher-dimensional mixed-integer program, and we characterize its convex hull in the original lower-dimensional space via a new class of inequalities called scenario recourse inequalities (SRIs). We show that SRIs are valid for any recourse policy satisfying mild assumptions and are sufficient for formulating the VRPSD under a scenario-optimal recourse policy, where the recourse actions are chosen optimally for each scenario. Under this latter policy, we also show that SRIs dominate several ILS cuts. We conduct computational experiments on the VRPSD with scenarios under both the classical and the scenario-optimal recourse policies. By using the SRIs, our algorithm solves 329 more instances to optimality than the previous state-of-the-art ILS algorithm.

</details>


### [69] [Optimal Projection-Free Adaptive SGD for Matrix Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02505)
*Dmitry Kovalev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文改进了Leon算法（One-sided Shampoo的实用变体）的分析，证明了其预条件子的稳定性，避免了额外超参数调优，并首次开发了具有Nesterov加速且无需每步投影的实用One-sided Shampoo变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Leon算法分析存在两个主要问题：1) 需要在预条件子中调优额外超参数；2) 在超出有界梯度假设的凸优化问题中无法获得维度无关的收敛保证。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 通过证明Leon预条件子的稳定性性质，改进算法分析。基于此分析，避免了额外超参数调优，并开发了首个具有Nesterov加速且无需每步投影的实用One-sided Shampoo变体。同时提供了非光滑非凸设置下的改进维度无关收敛率，并对所提算法进行了统一分析。

Result: 1) 避免了Leon算法中额外超参数的调优；2) 开发了首个具有Nesterov加速且无需每步投影的实用One-sided Shampoo变体；3) 在非光滑非凸设置下获得了改进的维度无关收敛率；4) 提供了加速投影自由自适应SGD与（块）对角预条件子的统一分析框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过改进Leon预条件子的稳定性分析，解决了现有算法需要额外超参数调优和缺乏维度无关收敛保证的问题，成功开发了首个具有Nesterov加速且无需每步投影的实用One-sided Shampoo变体，为自适应优化算法提供了更高效的理论和实践方案。

Abstract: Recently, Jiang et al. [2026] developed Leon, a practical variant of One-sided Shampoo [Xie et al., 2025a, An et al., 2025] algorithm for online convex optimization, which does not require computing a costly quadratic projection at each iteration. Unfortunately, according to the existing analysis, Leon requires tuning an additional hyperparameter in its preconditioner and cannot achieve dimension-independent convergence guarantees for convex optimization problems beyond the bounded gradients assumption. In this paper, we resolve this issue by proving certain stability properties of Leon's preconditioner. Using our improved analysis, we show that tuning the extra hyperparameter can be avoided and, more importantly, develop the first practical variant of One-sided Shampoo with Nesterov acceleration, which does not require computing projections at each iteration. As a side contribution, we obtain improved dimension-independent rates in the non-smooth non-convex setting and develop a unified analysis of the proposed algorithm, which yields accelerated projection-free adaptive SGD with (block-)diagonal preconditioners.

</details>


### [70] [Observer-Based Performance-Barrier Event-Triggered Control of $2\times2$ Linear Hyperbolic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02508)
*Eranda Somathilake,Mamadou Diagne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输出反馈的动态性能障碍事件触发控制方法，用于2×2线性双曲型PDE系统的边界控制，在保持系统性能的同时延长事件间隔时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能障碍事件触发控制方法需要全状态信息，限制了其在输出反馈场景下的应用。本文旨在解决这一限制，为具有反共位测量的双曲型PDE系统开发输出反馈下的动态性能障碍事件触发控制方案。

Method: 提出一种结合指数收敛观测器的动态性能障碍事件触发控制机制。该方法考虑2×2线性双曲型PDE系统的边界控制问题，控制输入与测量反共位。通过性能残差评估系统性能，允许Lyapunov函数非单调递减，从而延长事件间隔时间。

Result: 证明了所提机制存在最小驻留时间，并证明了系统解的L^2范数全局指数稳定性。仿真结果验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 成功将性能障碍事件触发控制扩展到输出反馈场景，为具有反共位测量的双曲型PDE系统提供了一种有效的边界控制方法，在保持系统性能的同时显著减少了控制更新频率。

Abstract: Performance-barrier event-triggered control (P-ETC) is a methodology implemented to increase the dwell-times between events while still preserving a prescribed performance of the system under event-triggered control (ETC). This is achieved by considering the performance residual of the system, which is a measure of the system performance with respect to the prescribed performance. This allows the Lyapunov function candidate to deviate from decreasing monotonically. In order to determine the performance residual, it is required to know the full-state information, leading to all work related to P-ETC to be under full-state feedback. In this article, we propose a novel dynamic performance-barrier under output feedback with an exponentially convergent observer. We consider event-triggered boundary control of a class of $2\times2$ linear hyperbolic PDEs with anti-collocated measurements with the control input. Under the proposed P-ETC mechanism, we prove the existence of a minimum dwell-time, and show the global exponential stability of the spatial $L^2$ norm of the solution of the system. Simulation results are presented to validate the theoretical claims.

</details>


### [71] [Moreau Envelope-Based Clustering for Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02540)
*Nguyen Thi Thu Van*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Moreau包络的高效数据聚类算法，自动确定聚类数量，通过平滑化将原始问题转化为结构化优化任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义多源Weber问题中的非光滑非凸分量，需要自动确定聚类数量，提高聚类效率和可扩展性

Method: 使用Moreau包络平滑非光滑非凸分量，将问题转化为结构化优化任务，采用一阶方法和简单矩阵向量运算，通过逐步移除空或冗余聚类自动确定聚类数量

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的数值实验表明，该方法快速、可扩展，在聚类质量和计算效率方面与现有方法具有竞争力

Conclusion: 基于Moreau包络的聚类算法能有效处理非光滑非凸问题，自动确定聚类数量，具有高效性和可扩展性

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an efficient algorithm for data clustering based on the Moreau envelope, which approximates nonsmooth and nonconvex components of the generalized multi-source Weber problem. The number of clusters is not fixed in advance and is determined automatically by progressively removing empty or redundant clusters. The smoothing induced by the Moreau envelope transforms the original problem into a structured optimization task that can be efficiently solved using first-order methods and simple matrix vector operations. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the proposed approach is fast, scalable, and competitive with existing methods in both clustering quality and computational efficiency.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate a distributed optimal control problem for the viscous Camassa--Holm equations with sparse controls and a general cost functional. Considering three different forms of sparsity-promoting terms, we prove the existence of optimal solutions, derive the corresponding optimality conditions and analyze the stability of optimal solutions with respect to the sparsity parameter.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种新的等式约束优化方法，旨在实现全局收敛到二阶稳定点、局部二次收敛，并获得该问题类别中最优的复杂度保证

Method: 基于序列三次规划框架，每步迭代利用约束雅可比分解为法向和切向分量，切向分量通过求解涉及三次正则化的子问题获得，必要时引入二阶校正步骤

Result: 方法能全局收敛到二阶稳定点并实现局部二次收敛，首次获得拉格朗日梯度复杂度O(ε_g^{-3/2})、二阶稳定性O(ε_H^{-3})和约束违反O(ε_c^{-1})的最坏情况复杂度保证

Conclusion: 该方法在等式约束优化问题中实现了最优的复杂度保证，是该问题类别中已知的最佳复杂度结果

Abstract: We develop a new method for equality constrained optimization problems based on a sequential cubic programming framework. Each iteration utilizes a step decomposition based on the Jacobian of the constraints into a normal and a tangential component, the latter of which is found by solving a subproblem involving cubic regularization. The method incorporates second-order correction steps as necessary to ensure global convergence to second-order stationary points as well as local quadratic convergence. In addition, we show that the algorithm is the first to obtain worst case complexity guarantees on the order of $\mathcal{O}(ε_g^{-3/2})$ for the gradient of the Lagrangian, $\mathcal{O}(ε_H^{-3})$ in terms of second-order stationarity, and $\mathcal{O}(ε_c^{-1})$ in terms of the constraint violation. These are the best known complexity guarantees of any method for this class of problems.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于一类无约束一阶算法的规范结构，该结构具有Lur'e表示，包括相对阶大于1的系统（如带延迟梯度反馈的系统）。通过线性变换获得该结构，并可通过Lyapunov诱导范数中的投影将无约束优化算法直接扩展到集合约束算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无约束一阶优化算法（包括相对阶大于1的系统，如带延迟梯度反馈的系统）缺乏统一的规范结构。这种统一结构的缺失限制了从无约束算法到集合约束算法的直接扩展，无法在保持收敛速率的同时处理约束优化问题。

Method: 通过简单的线性变换，为具有Lur'e表示的一类无约束一阶算法建立规范结构。利用该结构，在Lyapunov诱导范数中引入投影操作，将无约束算法直接扩展到集合约束情况。投影在Lyapunov诱导范数中执行，确保算法收敛性。

Result: 提出的投影算法能够达到最优解，同时保持其无约束对应算法的收敛速率。规范结构使得具有相对阶大于1的系统（如带延迟梯度反馈的系统）也能通过投影方法处理集合约束。

Conclusion: 建立了一类无约束一阶算法的规范结构，通过Lyapunov诱导范数中的投影实现了从无约束到集合约束算法的直接扩展。该方法在保持收敛速率的同时处理约束优化问题，适用于包括相对阶大于1的系统在内的广泛算法类别。

Abstract: This work introduces a canonical structure for a broad class of unconstrained first-order algorithms that admit a Lur'e representation, including systems with relative degree greater than one, e.g., systems with delayed gradient feedback. The proposed canonical structure is obtained through a simple linear transformation. It enables a direct extension from unconstrained optimization algorithms to set-constrained ones through projection in a Lyapunov-induced norm. The resulting projected algorithms attain the optimal solution while preserving the convergence rates of their unconstrained counterparts.

</details>


### [75] [Adaptive Newton-CG methods with global and local analysis for unconstrained optimization with Hölder continuous Hessian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02763)
*Ziyang Zeng,Junyu Zhang,Chuan He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出两种自适应正则化的Newton-CG算法，用于求解Hölder连续Hessian的非凸优化问题，避免嵌套线搜索并保持局部超线性收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Newton-CG方法存在两个主要问题：(1) 非自适应正则化导致早期进展效率低下且失去局部超线性收敛性；(2) 嵌套线搜索过程每次迭代需要多次求解牛顿系统，计算成本高

Method: 提出两种新型Newton-CG算法，根据是否已知Hölder指数ν，采用自适应正则化技术，利用auto-conditioning方法消除嵌套线搜索，自适应调整正则化参数

Result: 算法达到了已知最优迭代复杂度O(H_f^{1/(1+ν)}ε^{-(2+ν)/(1+ν)})以找到ε-平稳点，同时在非退化局部极小点附近保持局部超线性收敛

Conclusion: 提出的自适应正则化Newton-CG算法克服了现有方法的局限性，在理论收敛性和计算效率方面均有显著改进，数值实验验证了其相对于现有方法的实际优势

Abstract: In this paper, we study Newton-conjugate gradient (Newton-CG) methods for minimizing a nonconvex function $f$ whose Hessian is $(H_f,ν)$-Hölder continuous with modulus $H_f>0$ and exponent $ν\in(0,1]$. Recently proposed Newton-CG methods for this problem adopt (i) non-adaptive regularization and (ii) a nested line-search procedure, where (i) often leads to inefficient early progress and the loss of local superlinear convergence, and (ii) may incur high computational cost due to multiple solves of the Newton system per iteration. To address these limitations, we propose two novel Newton-CG algorithms, depending on the availability of $ν$, that adaptively regularize the Newton system by leveraging the auto-conditioning technique to eliminate the nested line search. The proposed algorithms achieve the best-known iteration complexity ${\mathcal O}\big(H_f^{1/(1+ν)}ε^{-(2+ν)/(1+ν)}\big)$ for finding an $ε$-stationary point and, simultaneously, enjoy local superlinear convergence near nondegenerate local minimizers. Numerical experiments further demonstrate the practical advantages of our algorithms over existing approaches.

</details>


### [76] [Computing the Pareto Front by Polynomial Elimination, With an Application From System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02806)
*Hans van Rooij,Christof Vermeersch,Marie Deferme,Bart De Moor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于消元法计算多元多项式多目标优化问题帕累托前沿的数值方法，将帕累托前沿表示为目标空间中的代数簇


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于采样的方法只能逐点近似帕累托前沿，需要预先确定采样点数量，无法提供帕累托前沿的显式代数描述

Method: 将目标函数与权重结合，将权重作为额外决策变量，通过变量消元消除除目标值外的所有变量，得到描述帕累托前沿的多项式方程

Result: 该方法能够将帕累托前沿表示为目标空间中的几何对象，无需预先确定采样点数量，在系统辨识等应用中展示了有效性

Conclusion: 消元法为多项式多目标优化问题提供了一种新的帕累托前沿计算方法，能够获得帕累托前沿的显式代数描述，优于传统的采样方法

Abstract: We propose a novel numerical approach to compute the Pareto front in multivariate polynomial multi-objective optimization problems. When the objective functions and (equality) constraints are multivariate polynomials, the Pareto front, which describes the efficient points of the multiple (often conflicting) objective functions, can be interpreted as a subset of a positive-dimensional algebraic variety. By combining the objective functions with weights and considering the weights as additional decision variables, we can eliminate all variables except the objective values and obtain one (or multiple) polynomial equation(s) that describes the Pareto front. Unlike sampling-based methods that approximate the Pareto front point-wise, our elimination-based approach yields an explicit algebraic relation between the objective values, representing the Pareto front as a geometric object in the objective space without requiring a predetermined number of sample points. Besides numerical examples illustrating the elimination-based approach, we use elimination on a challenging application that originates from system identification, in which we analyze the trade-off between misfit and latency terms when determining the optimal model parameters from measured data.

</details>


### [77] [Random Reshuffling-Based Distributed Nash Equilibrium Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02858)
*Jun Hu,Chao Sun,Chen Bo,Jianzheng Wang,Zheming Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于随机重排(RR)的分布式纳什均衡求解方法，针对非合作博弈问题，相比传统有放回采样方法，RR采用无放回组件更新，在分布式部分决策信息设置下实现线性收敛到纳什均衡邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式随机纳什均衡方法主要依赖有放回采样，而随机重排(RR)的无放回采样在优化问题中表现出优越性能，但尚未在分布式博弈均衡求解中得到系统研究。论文旨在将RR的优势扩展到分布式纳什均衡求解领域。

Method: 提出基于随机重排的分布式纳什均衡求解框架：1) 建立全信息基准，引入中间参考轨迹和重排方差来刻画RR引起的epoch-wise动态；2) 扩展到部分决策信息设置，每个玩家使用联合动作配置的局部估计更新其动作；3) 分析常数参数下的线性收敛性和递减参数下的精确收敛性。

Result: 全信息情况下建立了RR迭代的下降型边界；分布式部分决策信息情况下，常数参数下算法线性收敛到纳什均衡邻域，递减参数下几乎必然且均方收敛到精确纳什均衡。在EV充电博弈和非二次边资源准入博弈的数值实验中，RR在稳态精度和长时域性能上均优于传统有放回SGD基线。

Conclusion: 随机重排(RR)为分布式纳什均衡求解提供了有效的无放回采样框架，在理论和实验上均优于传统有放回方法，特别适用于样本平均近似的随机博弈问题，为分布式博弈均衡计算提供了新的算法范式。

Abstract: This paper studies random reshuffling (RR)-based distributed Nash equilibrium seeking for noncooperative games. The game is motivated as a sample-average approximation of an underlying expected-value stochastic game, while the algorithmic focus is placed on the resulting finite-sum equilibrium problem. Unlike existing distributed stochastic Nash equilibrium methods that mainly rely on with-replacement sampling, the proposed approach incorporates without-replacement component updates into equilibrium computation over networks. We first consider a full-information benchmark, for which an intermediate reference trajectory and a shuffling variance are introduced to characterize the epoch-wise dynamics induced by RR. The method is then extended to the more practical partial-decision-information setting, where each player updates its action using local estimates of the joint action profile. For the full-information case, a descent-type bound is established for the RR iterates. For the distributed partial-decision-information case, it is shown that, under constant parameters, the proposed algorithm converges linearly to a neighborhood of the Nash equilibrium, while under diminishing parameters, it converges exactly to the Nash equilibrium almost surely and in mean square. Numerical experiments on an EV charging game and a nonquadratic edge resource admission game demonstrate that RR consistently outperforms the conventional with-replacement SGD baseline in both steady-state accuracy and long-horizon performance.

</details>


### [78] [Importance Sampling Optimization with Laplace Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02882)
*Radu-Alexandru Dragomir,François Portier,Victor Priser*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于重要性采样的超参数调优后处理方法，通过对评估过的配置进行加权平均而非选择单点最优，无需额外函数评估即可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格搜索和随机搜索是机器学习中广泛使用的超参数调优方法，但这些方法只选择单个最佳配置，可能无法充分利用已有评估信息。需要一种简单且计算成本低的方法来改进这一范式。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) 后处理方法：在随机搜索基础上，使用基于拉普拉斯原理的重要性采样方案对已评估配置进行加权平均；2) 迭代变体：自适应选择采样分布生成新候选点，类似进化策略方法。

Result: 理论分析表明，在n次评估后，所提方法的误差阶数为n^{-2/(d+2)}，优于随机搜索或网格搜索的n^{-1/d}（当d>2时）。在多个实际示例中验证了该平均策略的实用优势。

Conclusion: 提出的加权平均方法作为随机搜索的后处理步骤，无需额外函数评估即可显著提升超参数调优性能，特别在高维问题中优势明显，为传统调优方法提供了简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Grid search and random search are widely used techniques for hyperparameter tuning in machine learning, especially when gradient information is unavailable. In these methods, a finite set of candidate configurations is evaluated, and the best-performing one is selected. We propose a simple and computationally inexpensive refinement of this paradigm: instead of selecting a single best point, we form a weighted average of the evaluated configurations, where the weights are chosen using an importance sampling scheme inspired by the Laplace principle. This scheme can be implemented as a post-processing step on top of a random search, with no additional function evaluations. We also propose an iterative variant, where the sampling distributions are chosen adaptively to generate new candidate points around the previous estimate, in the spirit of Evolution Strategy (ES) methods. In a general non-convex setting, we show that, after n evaluations, the error of the proposed methods is of smaller order than n -2/(d+2) . This compares favorably to random search or grid search rates of n -1/d as soon as d > 2. We illustrate the practical benefits of this averaging strategy on several examples.

</details>

Result: 建立了统一的框架，将路由和套利整合为单一问题；扩展了现有算法以处理负输入情况

Abstract: We extend AMM trade functions to negative inputs via the \textit{concave continuation}, derived from the invariance of the local conservation law under allocation direction flips. This unifies routing and arbitrage into a single problem. We extend the one-hop transfer algorithm proposed in \cite{jiang} to this setting.

</details>


### [80] [Memory-Type Null Controllability for Non-Autonomous Degenerate Parabolic Equations with Boundary Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02916)
*Dev Prakash Jha,Raju K. George*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有Volterra型记忆项的一维非自治退化抛物方程的记忆型零能控性，建立了适用于非自治退化算子的Carleman估计，证明了在适当结构条件下的记忆型零能控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有记忆效应的退化抛物方程的零能控性问题。由于记忆项的存在，经典零能控性不足以保证系统完全归零，需要引入更强的概念同时要求状态和累积记忆项都消失。非自治系数和边界退化性增加了问题的复杂性。

Method: 建立适用于非自治退化算子的新Carleman估计，在加权空间中处理问题。将记忆项作为Carleman框架中的低阶扰动处理。通过Carleman估计推导合适的可观测性不等式，进而证明记忆型零能控性。

Result: 证明了在适当结构条件下，具有Volterra型记忆项的一维非自治退化抛物方程的记忆型零能控性。结果可扩展到双边界退化情形和移动控制区域。

Conclusion: 通过建立新的Carleman估计和可观测性不等式，成功解决了具有记忆效应的非自治退化抛物方程的记忆型零能控性问题，为这类复杂系统的控制理论提供了重要工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the memory-type null controllability of a class of one-dimensional non-autonomous degenerate parabolic equations with Volterra-type memory terms. The diffusion operator is considered in both divergence and non-divergence forms and may exhibit weak or strong degeneracy at the boundary, while the diffusion coefficient depends explicitly on time. Due to the presence of memory effects, classical null controllability is insufficient, and a stronger notion requiring the vanishing of both the state and the accumulated memory is introduced. To address this problem, we establish new Carleman estimates adapted to non-autonomous degenerate operators in weighted spaces. The memory term is handled as a lower-order perturbation within the Carleman framework. These estimates yield suitable observability inequalities, which allow us to prove memory-type null controllability under appropriate structural conditions. Extensions to cases with double boundary degeneracy and moving control regions are also discussed.

</details>
