### [81] [Scalable Mean-Variance Portfolio Optimization via Subspace Embeddings and GPU-Friendly Nesterov-Accelerated Projected Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02917)
*Yi-Shuai Niu,Yajuan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于草图因子降维和Nesterov加速投影梯度算法(NPGA)的双重加速求解器，用于大规模约束均值-方差投资组合优化，通过GPU加速显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模投资组合优化面临高维协方差矩阵计算复杂度高的问题，传统求解器如Gurobi在处理大规模问题时计算时间过长，需要开发更高效的算法来降低计算成本。

Method: 结合随机子空间嵌入、谱截断和岭稳定化构建有效因子L_eff，采用Nesterov加速投影梯度算法(NPGA)配合GPU友好的矩阵-向量核，通过标量对偶搜索计算结构化投影。

Result: 在5440个资产的真实数据基准测试中，NPGA-GPU在2.80秒内求解完整模型，而Gurobi需要64.84秒；优化后的压缩GPU变体保持在低个位数秒级别，同时保持目标精度。

Conclusion: 该方法证明完整稠密模型在现代GPU上已实用，压缩后的主要瓶颈是投影而非矩阵-向量乘法，为大规模投资组合优化提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We develop a sketch-based factor reduction and a Nesterov-accelerated projected gradient algorithm (NPGA) with GPU acceleration, yielding a doubly accelerated solver for large-scale constrained mean-variance portfolio optimization. Starting from the sample covariance factor $L$, the method combines randomized subspace embedding, spectral truncation, and ridge stabilization to construct an effective factor $L_{eff}$. It then solves the resulting constrained problem with a structured projection computed by scalar dual search and GPU-friendly matrix-vector kernels, yielding one computational pipeline for the baseline, sketched, and Sketch-Truncate-Ridge (STR)-regularized models. We also establish approximation, conditioning, and stability guarantees for the sketching and STR models, including explicit $O(\varepsilon)$ bounds for the covariance approximation, the optimal value error, and the solution perturbation under $(\varepsilon,δ)$-subspace embeddings. Experiments on synthetic and real equity-return data show that the method preserves objective accuracy while reducing runtime substantially. On a 5440-asset real-data benchmark with 48374 training periods, NPGA-GPU solves the unreduced full model in 2.80 seconds versus 64.84 seconds for Gurobi, while the optimized compressed GPU variants remain in the low-single-digit-second regime. These results show that the full dense model is already practical on modern GPUs and that, after compression, the remaining bottleneck is projection rather than matrix-vector multiplication.

</details>


### [82] [Stabilized Proximal Point Method via Trust Region Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02943)
*Hanmin Li,Kaja Gruntkowska,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种信任域稳定化近端点方法，通过局部可行域计算近端更新，在缺乏强凸性假设下实现线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近端点方法(PPM)在一般凸性假设下缺乏强凸性或结构假设时无法实现线性收敛，需要一种稳定化机制来保证非零步长和线性下降。

Method: 提出信任域稳定化近端点方案，在每个近端更新步骤中限制在局部可行区域内计算。通过两种参数机制保证线性下降：固定信任域半径并适当选择正则化参数，或固定正则化参数并通过均匀位移下界选择半径。

Result: 该方法在任意指定邻域外实现目标值的线性下降，无需光滑性或强凸性假设。识别位移条件是线性下降的关键驱动因素，并建立了与Broximal Point Method在主动约束机制下的精确等价性。

Conclusion: 信任域稳定化是增强近端点方法收敛性的有效机制，在强凸性下信任域变得冗余，该方法为无强凸性假设的非光滑凸优化提供了线性收敛保证。

Abstract: The Proximal Point Method (PPM) (Rockafellar, 1976) is a fundamental tool for nonsmooth convex optimization. However, its convergence is not linear under general convexity in the absence of strong convexity or other structural assumptions. To address this limitation, we study a trust-region stabilized proximal point scheme in which each proximal update is computed over a localized feasible region. We show that this simple stabilization enforces non-vanishing steps and yields a linear decrease in objective values outside any prescribed neighborhood, without assuming smoothness or strong convexity. Our analysis identifies a displacement condition as the key driver of linear descent and provides two complementary parameter regimes to guarantee it: fixing the trust-region radius and choosing the regularization properly, or fixing the regularization and selecting radii via a uniform displacement lower bound. We further give explicit characterization of the linear regime conditions respectively, and prove that the trust-region is redundant under strong convexity, Finally, we establish an exact equivalence with the Broximal Point Method (BPM) (Gruntkowska et al., 2025) in the active constraint regime.

</details>


### [83] [Separable QCQPs and Their Exact SDP Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02968)
*Masakazu Kojima,Sunyoung Kim,Naohiko Arima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究可分离二次约束二次规划(QCQP)的精确半定规划松弛(SDPR)，提出通过可分离水平连接组合多个QCQP时保持精确性的框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何从多个具有精确SDPR的QCQP构建更大的可分离QCQP，并确保精确性在组合过程中得以保持

Method: 提出可分离水平连接框架，其中耦合通过约束的右侧参数实现，建立保持精确性的充分条件

Result: 证明该条件适用于凸QCQP、由符号模式和图结构条件定义的QCQP、以及约束数量有限的可分离齐次QCQP

Conclusion: 该框架具有构造性，可通过组合异构QCQP生成具有精确SDP松弛的新实例

Abstract: This paper studies exact semidefinite programming relaxations (SDPRs) for separable quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs). We consider the construction of a larger separable QCQP from multiple QCQPs with exact SDPRs. We show that exactness is preserved when such QCQPs are combined through a separable horizontal connection, where the coupling is induced through the right-hand-side parameters of the constraints. The proposed framework provides a simple sufficient condition for exactness of the resulting SDPR. We then identify notable classes of QCQPs for which this condition holds, including convex QCQPs, QCQPs defined by sign-pattern and graph-structural conditions, and separable homogeneous QCQPs with a limited number of constraints. Two examples illustrate the constructive nature of the proposed framework, showing how heterogeneous QCQPs can be combined to yield new instances with exact SDP relaxations.

</details>


### [84] [The Quadratic Bin Packing Problem: Exact Formulations and Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03078)
*Vítor Gomes Chagas,Alberto Locatelli,Flávio Keidi Miyazawa,Manuel Iori*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了二次装箱问题(QBPP)，提出了三种紧凑MILP模型和集合划分公式，开发了分支定价算法，在基准实例上验证了分支定价算法的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QBPP在经典装箱问题基础上增加了每个箱子的固定成本和物品配对成本，具有重要的理论意义和实际应用价值，特别是在聚类分析领域。

Method: 提出了三种紧凑混合整数线性规划(MILP)公式和集合划分公式；为每个紧凑模型开发了增强版本以加强连续松弛；为集合划分公式设计了定制化的分支定价算法。

Result: 计算实验表明，增强的紧凑公式能有效求解小规模实例，而分支定价算法在整体性能上更优，特别是在更大、更具挑战性的实例上表现突出。

Conclusion: 分支定价算法是解决QBPP的有效方法，尤其适用于大规模复杂实例，而紧凑MILP公式适用于小规模问题求解。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce and study the Quadratic Bin Packing Problem (QBPP), which generalizes the classical bin packing problem by introducing a fixed cost for each used bin and a pairwise cost (or profit) incurred whenever two items are packed together. Beyond its theoretical relevance, the QBPP is of practical interest due to its numerous real-world applications, mainly related to cluster analysis. To address the QBPP, we propose three compact mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulations, along with a set-partitioning formulation. For each compact model, we present an enhanced version with a strengthened continuous relaxation, while, for the set-partitioning formulation, we develop a tailored Branch-and-Price algorithm. Computational experiments on benchmark instances demonstrated that, while the enhanced compact formulations can be effectively solved by a standard MILP solver for small-sized instances, the Branch-and-Price approach delivered superior performance overall, especially on larger and more challenging instances.

</details>


### [85] [Swarm-Based Inertial Methods for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03124)
*Qiyu Wu,Kunhui Luan,Qi Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于耗散惯性动力学系统的群体优化方法，通过梯度与Hessian信息调整阻尼机制，实现快速收敛到全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体优化方法缺乏系统性的能量耗散理论框架，需要开发能够自适应调整阻尼、利用曲率信息、并保证能量耗散特性的新型惯性动力学方法。

Method: 基于广义Onsager原理构建耦合耗散惯性动力学系统，提出包含梯度与Hessian信息的欠阻尼惯性动力学，设计保持结构特性的离散化方案和多种高效数值算法。

Result: 理论证明系统满足能量耗散定律，获得目标函数与全局最小值间隙的渐近衰减上界O(1/δ(t))，数值实验显示收敛速度远超理论保证，在非凸问题上达到高成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作从能量耗散视角为群体惯性方法提供了系统性的构建与分析框架，所提方法在凸与非凸问题上均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of swarm-based inertial methods (SBIMs) for global minimization, formulated as coupled dissipative inertial dynamical systems derived from the generalized Onsager principle. The proposed framework identifies the friction operator and the scaling of the potential energy, namely the objective function to be minimized, as the key ingredients governing relaxation dynamics over the energy landscape. Within this framework, we propose a new underdamped inertial dynamics whose damping mechanisms incorporate both gradient and Hessian information, allowing the system to adjust damping or acceleration according to the agent trajectories and the curvature of the landscape. Under suitable conditions, we prove that the underdamped system satisfies an energy dissipation law, from which we establish an upper bound on the asymptotic decay rate of the gap between the objective function and its global minimum, given by $O(1/δ(t))$ (defined in §3). We further construct structure-preserving discretizations that retain both discrete energy dissipation and the convergence rate estimate, $O(1/δ_k)$ (defined in \S3). In addition, we present several other efficient numerical algorithms for the dynamical system. Numerical experiments for all proposed algorithms validate the theory on convex test problems and demonstrate convergence rates in function values that are substantially faster than the theoretical guarantees ($O(1/δ_k)$). On nonconvex benchmark problems, the proposed methods achieve high success rates in reaching the global minimum, and exhibit more stable energy decay than swarm-based gradient descent and Nesterov methods. Overall, this work provides a systematic framework for the construction and analysis of SBIMs from an energy-dissipative perspective.

</details>


### [86] [Observer design for classes of nonlinear port-Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03151)
*Filippo Ugolini,Ning Liu,Yongxin Wu,Yann Le Gorrec,Alessandro Macchelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对具有状态依赖输入矩阵的端口哈密顿系统的系统化观测器设计方法，使用LPV多面体嵌入框架和LMI综合条件，实现了指数收敛的观测器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机电系统（如磁悬浮系统、MEMS器件、电活性聚合物致动器等）在准静态电气假设下建模时，会自然产生状态依赖的输入矩阵。现有方法对此类系统的观测器设计存在局限性，需要开发系统化的设计方法。

Method: 采用LPV多面体嵌入框架和基于LMI的综合条件。通过积分中值表示将非线性误差动力学表示为线性顶点系统的凸组合，从而系统化计算确保指数收敛的观测器增益。推导了常增益和增益调度两种观测器设计。

Result: 数值结果表明所提观测器有效，增益调度设计相比常增益方法显著提高了最大可认证衰减率，减少了保守性。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种针对具有状态依赖输入矩阵的端口哈密顿系统的系统化观测器设计方法，通过LPV嵌入和LMI综合实现了指数收敛，增益调度设计相比常增益方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic observer design methodology for a class of port-Hamiltonian (pH) systems with state-dependent input matrices. Such systems can model a wide range of electromechanical systems, including magnetic levitation systems, MEMS devices, and electro-active polymer actuators such as DEA actuators, HASEL actuators, etc. In these applications, state-dependent input matrices naturally arise when the system is modeled under quasi-static electrical assumptions. An LPV polytopic embedding framework, together with LMI-based synthesis conditions, is proposed. The nonlinear error dynamics are represented as a convex combination of linear vertex systems using an integral mean value representation, which enables systematic computation of the observer gains that ensures exponential convergence. Both constant-gain and gain-scheduled observers are derived. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed observer, with the gain-scheduled design achieving a significant increase in the maximum certifiable decay rate compared with constant-gain approaches, thereby reducing conservatism.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Generating Counterfactual Patient Timelines from Real-World Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02337)
*Yu Akagi,Tomohisa Seki,Toru Takiguchi,Hiromasa Ito,Yoshimasa Kawazoe,Kazuhiko Ohe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于30万患者、4亿条时间线数据的自回归生成模型能够生成临床合理的反事实轨迹，用于模拟不同临床场景下的患者结局


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟在个性化医疗和计算机临床试验中具有重要应用前景，但现有方法存在局限性，需要开发能够基于真实世界数据生成临床合理反事实轨迹的新方法

Method: 使用超过30万患者和4亿条患者时间线条目的真实世界数据训练自回归生成模型，以自监督方式学习临床轨迹模式，然后应用于COVID-19住院患者，通过修改年龄、C反应蛋白和肌酐等变量来模拟7天结局

Result: 反事实模拟显示：年龄增大、C反应蛋白升高和肌酐升高与住院死亡率增加相关；C反应蛋白升高时瑞德西韦处方增加，肾功能受损时处方减少；这些反事实轨迹再现了已知的临床模式

Conclusion: 基于真实世界数据以自监督方式训练的自回归生成模型能够为反事实临床模拟奠定基础，具有临床应用潜力

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation - exploring hypothetical consequences under alternative clinical scenarios - holds promise for transformative applications such as personalized medicine and in silico trials. However, it remains challenging due to methodological limitations. Here, we show that an autoregressive generative model trained on real-world data from over 300,000 patients and 400 million patient timeline entries can generate clinically plausible counterfactual trajectories. As a validation task, we applied the model to patients hospitalized with COVID-19 in 2023, modifying age, serum C-reactive protein (CRP), and serum creatinine to simulate 7-day outcomes. Increased in-hospital mortality was observed in counterfactual simulations with older age, elevated CRP, and elevated serum creatinine. Remdesivir prescriptions increased in simulations with higher CRP values and decreased in those with impaired kidney function. These counterfactual trajectories reproduced known clinical patterns. These findings suggest that autoregressive generative models trained on real-world data in a self-supervised manner can establish a foundation for counterfactual clinical simulation.

</details>


### [88] [LiME: Lightweight Mixture of Experts for Efficient Multimodal Multi-task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02338)
*Md Kowsher,Haris Mansoor,Nusrat Jahan Prottasha,Ozlem Garibay,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiME提出轻量级专家混合方法，通过单一共享PEFT模块和轻量调制向量实现专家专业化，无需为每个专家复制适配器，显著减少可训练参数并提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-PEFT方法需要为每个专家单独配置适配器，导致可训练参数随专家数量线性增长，且仅限于适配器架构。需要一种更轻量、通用的方法来实现多任务适应。

Method: LiME采用单一共享PEFT模块，通过轻量级专家向量调制其输出实现专家专业化。引入零参数路由，利用现有冻结和适应表示替代学习路由参数。包含n-gram窗口路由和基于路由置信度的自适应专家选择（Auto Top-K）。

Result: 在MMT-47多模态多任务基准测试中，LiME在47个跨文本、图像和视频任务上取得竞争性或更优性能，同时使用最多4倍少的可训练参数和最多29%更快的训练速度。

Conclusion: LiME通过轻量调制和零参数路由机制，实现了高效的多任务适应，显著减少了参数开销并提升了训练效率，为MoE-PEFT方法提供了更通用和可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: MoE-PEFT methods combine Mixture of Experts with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for multi-task adaptation, but require separate adapters per expert causing trainable parameters to scale linearly with expert count and limiting applicability to adapter-based architectures. We propose LiME (Lightweight Mixture of Experts), which achieves expert specialization through lightweight modulation rather than adapter replication. Instead of separate adapters, LiME uses a single shared PEFT module and modulates its output with lightweight expert vectors, reducing expert parameters while generalizing to any PEFT method. Notably, LiME introduces zero-parameter routing by leveraging existing frozen and adapted representations eliminating learned router parameters typically required per layer. Theoretically, we prove that (i) more experts preserve more task-relevant information and (ii) modulation approximates full expert-specific PEFT with bounded error. LiME further incorporates n-gram windowed routing and adaptive expert selection (Auto Top-K) based on routing confidence. Experiments on MMT-47, a multimodal multi-task benchmark with 47 tasks spanning text, image, and video, demonstrate that LiME achieves competitive or superior performance while using up to 4x fewer trainable parameters and up to 29% faster training compared to corresponding MoE-PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [89] [SIEVE: Sample-Efficient Parametric Learning from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02339)
*Parth Asawa,Alexandros G. Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIEVE：一种从自然语言上下文进行样本高效参数学习的方法，仅需三个查询示例即可实现上下文蒸馏


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言上下文包含丰富的信号用于语言模型适应，但现有参数学习方法数据需求大且依赖高质量轨迹或自动验证器

Method: 提出SIEVE方法，使用SIEVE-GEN合成数据生成管道，基于上下文可分解的洞察，将合成查询与适用上下文配对而非全部上下文，然后通过上下文蒸馏将上下文内化到模型中

Result: 在需要上下文的推理设置中（包括自定义领域、RuleArena和One Book机器翻译任务），SIEVE仅用三个查询示例就优于先前的上下文蒸馏方法

Conclusion: SIEVE展示了如何从自然语言实现样本高效的参数学习，为上下文适应提供了一种数据需求极低的有效方法

Abstract: Natural language context-such as instructions, knowledge, or feedback-contains rich signal for adapting language models. While in-context learning provides adaptation via the prompt, parametric learning persists into model weights and can improve performance further, though is data hungry and heavily relies on either high-quality traces or automated verifiers. We propose SIEVE, a method for sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language context that requires as few as three query examples. SIEVE uses a novel synthetic data generation pipeline, SIEVE-GEN, that leverages the insight that context is decomposable. Decomposing context allows us to generate higher quality rollouts by pairing synthetic queries with only the applicable context rather than the entirety, then using context distillation to internalize context into the model. We evaluate in reasoning settings where context is necessary, including custom domains and the RuleArena and Machine Translation from One Book tasks. Our results show that SIEVE outperforms prior context distillation methods using just three query examples, demonstrating how to achieve sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language.

</details>


### [90] [Not All Denoising Steps Are Equal: Model Scheduling for Faster Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02340)
*Ivan Sedykh,Nikita Sorokin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）的加速采样方法，通过模型调度策略在部分去噪步骤中使用小模型替代大模型，以减少计算成本同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型在生成质量上已接近自回归语言模型，但其采样过程计算昂贵，需要多次全序列去噪步骤且无法利用KV缓存。需要寻找降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 提出模型调度策略，在去噪过程的不同步骤中，使用较小的MDLM替代完整模型。通过损失和KL散度分析确定各步骤的重要性，并基于此设计调度规则，重点关注中间步骤的敏感性。

Result: 在OpenWebText数据集上，实验表明早期和晚期去噪步骤对模型替换具有较强鲁棒性，而中间步骤最为敏感。通过合理调度可实现高达17%的FLOPs减少，同时生成困惑度仅有适度下降。

Conclusion: 简单的架构无关调度规则能显著加速MDLM采样，同时基本保持生成质量。中间扩散轨迹步骤对模型质量最为关键，而早期和晚期步骤可安全使用小模型替代。

Abstract: Recent advances in masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) narrow the quality gap to autoregressive LMs, but their sampling remains expensive because generation requires many full-sequence denoising passes with a large Transformer and, unlike autoregressive decoding, cannot benefit from KV caching. In this work, we exploit the flexibility of the diffusion framework and study model scheduling, where a smaller MDLM replaces the full model at a subset of denoising steps. On OpenWebText, we show that early and late denoising steps are substantially more robust to such replacement than middle steps, enabling up to a 17% reduction in FLOPs with only modest degradation in generative perplexity. We support these findings with a step-importance analysis based on loss and KL divergence between small and large models across timesteps, as well as an exhaustive search over coarse step segments, both of which identify the middle of the diffusion trajectory as most sensitive. Our results suggest that simple, architecture-agnostic scheduling rules can significantly accelerate MDLM sampling while largely preserving generation quality as measured by generative perplexity.

</details>


### [91] [LLM Reasoning with Process Rewards for Outcome-Guided Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02341)
*Mohammad Rezaei,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROGRS框架通过结果条件中心化处理过程奖励，将过程奖励作为结果组内的相对偏好而非绝对目标，结合冻结的分位数回归PRM和多尺度一致性评估器，在保持结果正确性主导的同时有效利用过程奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理强化学习方法主要优化最终结果正确性，但这对多步推理提供稀疏反馈。过程奖励模型(PRMs)虽然能评分中间步骤，但常与最终正确性不完全对齐，可能奖励局部流畅但最终错误的推理，导致奖励黑客攻击。

Method: 提出PROGRS框架：1) 结果条件中心化，将错误轨迹的PRM分数调整为组内零均值，消除系统偏差同时保留信息排名；2) 结合冻结分位数回归PRM和多尺度一致性评估器；3) 将中心化过程奖励整合到GRPO中，无需额外可训练组件。

Result: 在MATH-500、AMC、AIME、MinervaMath和OlympiadBench等数据集上，PROGRS相比仅优化结果的基线一致提升Pass@1性能，且用更少rollouts实现更强性能。

Conclusion: 结果条件中心化能够安全有效地利用过程奖励进行数学推理，在保持结果正确性主导的同时提供更密集的监督信号，避免奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning in large language models has improved substantially with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards, where final answers can be checked automatically and converted into reliable training signals. Most such pipelines optimize outcome correctness only, which yields sparse feedback for long, multi-step solutions and offers limited guidance on intermediate reasoning errors. Recent work therefore introduces process reward models (PRMs) to score intermediate steps and provide denser supervision. In practice, PRM scores are often imperfectly aligned with final correctness and can reward locally fluent reasoning that still ends in an incorrect answer. When optimized as absolute rewards, such signals can amplify fluent failure modes and induce reward hacking.
  We propose PROGRS, a framework that leverages PRMs while keeping outcome correctness dominant. PROGRS treats process rewards as relative preferences within outcome groups rather than absolute targets. We introduce outcome-conditioned centering, which shifts PRM scores of incorrect trajectories to have zero mean within each prompt group. It removes systematic bias while preserving informative rankings. PROGRS combines a frozen quantile-regression PRM with a multi-scale coherence evaluator. We integrate the resulting centered process bonus into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) without auxiliary objectives or additional trainable components. Across MATH-500, AMC, AIME, MinervaMath, and OlympiadBench, PROGRS consistently improves Pass@1 over outcome-only baselines and achieves stronger performance with fewer rollouts. These results show that outcome-conditioned centering enables safe and effective use of process rewards for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [92] [Haiku to Opus in Just 10 bits: LLMs Unlock Massive Compression Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02343)
*Roy Rinberg,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Simon Henniger,Nicholas Carlini,Keri Warr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM生成文本的压缩技术，包括无损和有损压缩，提出了一种压缩-计算权衡边界，并引入了基于问答的交互式有损压缩协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM生成文本的高效压缩方法，探索压缩与计算资源之间的权衡，旨在开发比传统传输完整响应更高效的知识传输协议。

Method: 1. 无损压缩：使用领域适应的LoRA适配器改进基于LLM的算术编码；2. 有损压缩：通过提示模型进行简洁重写后应用算术编码；3. 问答压缩(QA)：受"二十个问题"游戏启发，小模型通过向大模型询问是/否问题迭代优化响应，每次回答传输1比特信息。

Result: 1. 无损压缩：领域适应的LoRA适配器比仅使用基础LLM的压缩效果提升2倍；2. 有损压缩：简洁重写+算术编码实现约0.03的压缩比，比压缩原始响应提升2倍；3. QA压缩：在8个数学、科学和代码基准测试中，10个二进制问题可恢复小模型与大模型之间23%-72%的能力差距（标准基准）和7%-38%（更难基准），压缩比达到0.0006-0.004，比先前LLM压缩方法小100倍以上。

Conclusion: 交互式压缩协议（如QA压缩）比传输完整响应能更高效地传输知识，证明了压缩-计算权衡边界的存在，为LLM知识的高效传输提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study the compression of LLM-generated text across lossless and lossy regimes, characterizing a compression-compute frontier where more compression is possible at the cost of more compute. For lossless compression, domain-adapted LoRA adapters can improve LLM-based arithmetic coding by 2x over compression with the base LLM alone. For lossy compression, prompting a model for a succinct rewrite then applying arithmetic coding can achieve compression ratios of approximately 0.03, a 2x improvement over compressing the original response.
  We further introduce Question-Asking compression (QA), an interactive lossy protocol inspired by the game 'Twenty Questions'. A small model iteratively refines its response by asking yes/no questions to a stronger model, transferring exactly one bit per answer. On 8 benchmarks spanning math, science, and code, 10 binary questions recover 23% to 72% of the capability gap between a small and large model on standard benchmarks and 7% to 38% on harder benchmarks, achieving compression ratios of 0.0006 to 0.004. This is over 100x smaller than prior LLM-based compression (Deletang et al., 2024), suggesting that interactive protocols can transfer knowledge far more efficiently than transmitting full responses.

</details>


### [93] [UI-Oceanus: Scaling GUI Agents with Synthetic Environmental Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02345)
*Mengzhou Wu,Yuzhe Guo,Yuan Cao,Haochuan Lu,Songhe Zhu,Pingzhe Qu,Xin Chen,Kang Qin,Zhongpu Wang,Xiaode Zhang,Xinyi Wang,Wei Dai,Gang Cao,Yuetang Deng,Zhi Gong,Dezhi Ran,Linyi Li,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UI-Oceanus框架通过前向动力学预测界面状态变化，利用低成本自主探索生成监督数据，突破GUI智能体规模化的数据瓶颈和蒸馏天花板


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用GUI智能体的规模化受到两个主要限制：1) 昂贵人工演示的数据可扩展性瓶颈；2) 合成教师监督的"蒸馏天花板"。需要超越模仿高级轨迹的学习范式，转向基于环境反馈的交互物理掌握

Method: 提出UI-Oceanus框架，系统研究自监督目标，发现前向动力学（预测未来界面状态）是主要可扩展性驱动因素。利用低成本自主探索（经系统执行验证）转化为高密度生成监督，构建鲁棒内部世界模型

Result: 实验表明：1) 在合成动力学上进行持续预训练的模型比非CPT基线平均成功率提升7%（离线基准）；2) 真实世界在线导航中提升16.8%；3) 导航性能随合成数据量扩展；4) 前向预测建模显著优于逆推理

Conclusion: 基于前向预测建模的方法为GUI自动化提供了优越的可扩展路径，具有强大的跨域适应性和组合泛化能力，突破了传统基于模仿学习和蒸馏方法的局限性

Abstract: Scaling generalist GUI agents is hindered by the data scalability bottleneck of expensive human demonstrations and the "distillation ceiling" of synthetic teacher supervision. To transcend these limitations, we propose UI-Oceanus, a framework that shifts the learning focus from mimicking high-level trajectories to mastering interaction physics via ground-truth environmental feedback. Through a systematic investigation of self-supervised objectives, we identify that forward dynamics, defined as the generative prediction of future interface states, acts as the primary driver for scalability and significantly outweighs inverse inference. UI-Oceanus leverages this insight by converting low-cost autonomous exploration, which is verified directly by system execution, into high-density generative supervision to construct a robust internal world model. Experimental evaluations across a series of models demonstrate the decisive superiority of our approach: models utilizing Continual Pre-Training (CPT) on synthetic dynamics outperform non-CPT baselines with an average success rate improvement of 7% on offline benchmarks, which amplifies to a 16.8% gain in real-world online navigation. Furthermore, we observe that navigation performance scales with synthetic data volume. These results confirm that grounding agents in forward predictive modeling offers a superior pathway to scalable GUI automation with robust cross-domain adaptability and compositional generalization.

</details>


### [94] [DrugPlayGround: Benchmarking Large Language Models and Embeddings for Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02346)
*Tianyu Liu,Sihan Jiang,Fan Zhang,Kunyang Sun,Teresa Head-Gordon,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DrugPlayGround：一个用于评估和基准测试大语言模型在药物发现中性能的框架，专注于药物特性描述、药物协同作用、药物-蛋白质相互作用及生理反应预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在药物发现研究中展现出巨大潜力，但目前缺乏对其性能的客观评估，无法确定其相对于传统药物发现平台的优势和局限性

Method: 开发DrugPlayGround框架，用于评估和基准测试LLM在生成药物物理化学特性、药物协同作用、药物-蛋白质相互作用以及药物分子引入扰动后的生理反应等文本描述方面的性能。该框架与领域专家合作，为LLM预测提供详细解释

Result: 提出了一个系统性的评估框架，能够测试LLM在化学和生物推理能力方面的表现，推动LLM在药物发现各个阶段的前沿应用

Conclusion: DrugPlayGround为解决LLM在药物发现中缺乏客观评估的问题提供了解决方案，通过系统性的性能测试和专家解释机制，有望推动LLM在药物发现领域的更广泛应用

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are in the ascendancy for research in drug discovery, offering unprecedented opportunities to reshape drug research by accelerating hypothesis generation, optimizing candidate prioritization, and enabling more scalable and cost-effective drug discovery pipelines. However there is currently a lack of objective assessments of LLM performance to ascertain their advantages and limitations over traditional drug discovery platforms. To tackle this emergent problem, we have developed DrugPlayGround, a framework to evaluate and benchmark LLM performance for generating meaningful text-based descriptions of physiochemical drug characteristics, drug synergism, drug-protein interactions, and the physiological response to perturbations introduced by drug molecules. Moreover, DrugPlayGround is designed to work with domain experts to provide detailed explanations for justifying the predictions of LLMs, thereby testing LLMs for chemical and biological reasoning capabilities to push their greater use at the frontier of drug discovery at all of its stages.

</details>


### [95] [FTimeXer: Frequency-aware Time-series Transformer with Exogenous variables for Robust Carbon Footprint Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02347)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Zhou Long,Qingchang Ma,Hui Ma,Jinhai Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FTimeXer：一种频率感知的时间序列Transformer，通过FFT驱动的频率分支和门控时频融合来捕捉多尺度周期性，结合随机外生掩码和一致性正则化处理不规则外生输入，提升电网碳足迹预测的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网碳足迹的准确预测对产品碳足迹核算和脱碳决策至关重要。现有方法难以有效利用周期性振荡模式，且在面临不规则外生输入（如缺失数据、不对齐）时表现不佳。

Method: 提出FTimeXer模型：1）FFT驱动的频率分支结合门控时频融合，有效捕捉多尺度周期性；2）随机外生掩码与一致性正则化相结合，减少虚假相关性并增强稳定性；3）包含鲁棒训练方案以适应外生因素。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，FTimeXer相比强基线模型取得了持续改进，实现了更可靠的电网碳因子预测。

Conclusion: FTimeXer通过频率感知设计和鲁棒训练方案，有效解决了电网碳强度预测中的非平稳性、周期性利用不足以及外生输入不规则等问题，为产品碳足迹核算和脱碳决策提供了更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Accurate and up-to-date forecasting of the power grid's carbon footprint is crucial for effective product carbon footprint (PCF) accounting and informed decarbonization decisions. However, the carbon intensity of the grid exhibits high non-stationarity, and existing methods often struggle to effectively leverage periodic and oscillatory patterns. Furthermore, these methods tend to perform poorly when confronted with irregular exogenous inputs, such as missing data or misalignment. To tackle these challenges, we propose FTimeXer, a frequency-aware time-series Transformer designed with a robust training scheme that accommodates exogenous factors. FTimeXer features an Fast Fourier Transform (FFT)-driven frequency branch combined with gated time-frequency fusion, allowing it to capture multi-scale periodicity effectively. It also employs stochastic exogenous masking in conjunction with consistency regularization, which helps reduce spurious correlations and enhance stability. Experiments conducted on three real-world datasets show consistent improvements over strong baselines. As a result, these enhancements lead to more reliable forecasts of grid carbon factors, which are essential for effective PCF accounting and informed decision-making regarding decarbonization.

</details>


### [96] [Contextual Intelligence The Next Leap for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02348)
*André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出上下文强化学习新分类法，区分环境强加与智能体驱动因素，推动实现真正的上下文智能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习策略泛化能力有限，现有上下文处理方法将上下文视为单一静态观测，限制了智能体的泛化能力。需要更精细的上下文建模来提升智能体在真实世界中的部署效果。

Method: 提出新颖的上下文分类法，区分异源性（环境强加）和自源性（智能体驱动）因素。提出三个研究方向：1) 异构上下文学习；2) 多时间尺度建模；3) 抽象高层上下文集成。

Result: 构建了上下文作为一等建模原型的理论框架，为开发能够推理自身身份、世界约束及其演化的上下文感知智能体奠定基础。

Conclusion: 将上下文作为核心建模元素，能够催化新一代上下文感知智能体，使其能够在真实世界中安全高效地部署，实现真正的上下文智能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has produced spectacular results in games, robotics, and continuous control. Yet, despite these successes, learned policies often fail to generalize beyond their training distribution, limiting real-world impact. Recent work on contextual RL (cRL) shows that exposing agents to environment characteristics -- contexts -- can improve zero-shot transfer. So far, the community has treated context as a monolithic, static observable, an approach that constrains the generalization capabilities of RL agents.
  To achieve contextual intelligence we first propose a novel taxonomy of contexts that separates allogenic (environment-imposed) from autogenic (agent-driven) factors. We identify three fundamental research directions that must be addressed to promote truly contextual intelligence: (1) Learning with heterogeneous contexts to explicitly exploit the taxonomy levels so agents can reason about their influence on the world and vice versa; (2) Multi-time-scale modeling to recognize that allogenic variables evolve slowly or remain static, whereas autogenic variables may change within an episode, potentially requiring different learning mechanisms; (3) Integration of abstract, high-level contexts to incorporate roles, resource & regulatory regimes, uncertainties, and other non-physical descriptors that crucially influence behavior.
  We envision context as a first-class modeling primitive, empowering agents to reason about who they are, what the world permits, and how both evolve over time. By doing so, we aim to catalyze a new generation of context-aware agents that can be deployed safely and efficiently in the real world.

</details>


### [97] [OPRIDE: Offline Preference-based Reinforcement Learning via In-Dataset Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02349)
*Yiqin Yang,Hao Hu,Yihuan Mao,Jin Zhang,Chengjie Wu,Yuhua Jiang,Xu Yang,Runpeng Xie,Yi Fan,Bo Liu,Yang Gao,Bo Xu,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPRIDE算法通过最大化查询信息量的探索策略和折扣调度机制，显著提升离线偏好强化学习的查询效率，在多种任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好强化学习（PbRL）虽然能避免复杂的奖励设计并更好地与人类意图对齐，但获取人类偏好反馈的成本高昂且耗时，这成为PbRL应用的主要障碍。离线PbRL中查询效率低下的两个主要原因是：探索效率低下和奖励函数学习的过度优化。

Method: 提出了OPRIDE算法，包含两个关键特征：1）基于原则的探索策略，最大化查询的信息量；2）折扣调度机制，旨在缓解学习奖励函数的过度优化问题。

Result: 通过实证评估，OPRIDE显著优于现有方法，在显著减少查询数量的情况下实现了强大的性能。在各种运动、操作和导航任务上的实验结果验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: OPRIDE算法通过提升查询效率解决了离线偏好强化学习的关键瓶颈，为实际应用提供了更可行的解决方案，同时提供了算法的理论效率保证。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) can help avoid sophisticated reward designs and align better with human intentions, showing great promise in various real-world applications. However, obtaining human feedback for preferences can be expensive and time-consuming, which forms a strong barrier for PbRL. In this work, we address the problem of low query efficiency in offline PbRL, pinpointing two primary reasons: inefficient exploration and overoptimization of learned reward functions. In response to these challenges, we propose a novel algorithm, \textbf{O}ffline \textbf{P}b\textbf{R}L via \textbf{I}n-\textbf{D}ataset \textbf{E}xploration (OPRIDE), designed to enhance the query efficiency of offline PbRL. OPRIDE consists of two key features: a principled exploration strategy that maximizes the informativeness of the queries and a discount scheduling mechanism aimed at mitigating overoptimization of the learned reward functions. Through empirical evaluations, we demonstrate that OPRIDE significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving strong performance with notably fewer queries. Moreover, we provide theoretical guarantees of the algorithm's efficiency. Experimental results across various locomotion, manipulation, and navigation tasks underscore the efficacy and versatility of our approach.

</details>


### [98] [Modeling and Controlling Deployment Reliability under Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02351)
*Naimur Rahman,Naazreen Tabassum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个部署中心框架，将可靠性视为由区分度和校准度组成的动态状态，通过多目标控制问题平衡可靠性稳定性与干预成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在非平稳环境中部署时会面临时间分布漂移，这会随时间侵蚀预测可靠性。现有的缓解策略（如定期重训练和重新校准）通常关注孤立时间点的平均指标，未能显式建模可靠性在部署期间的演变过程。

Method: 提出部署中心框架，将可靠性视为由区分度和校准度组成的动态状态。该状态在连续评估窗口中的轨迹产生可测量的波动性概念，将部署适应问题表述为平衡可靠性稳定性与累积干预成本的多目标控制问题。定义了一族状态依赖的干预策略，并在大规模时间索引信用风险数据集上进行实证分析。

Result: 实验在包含135万笔贷款（2007-2018年）的大规模信用风险数据集上显示，选择性、漂移触发的干预策略能够比连续滚动重训练获得更平滑的可靠性轨迹，同时显著降低运营成本。

Conclusion: 这些发现将时间漂移下的部署可靠性定位为可控的多目标系统，并强调了策略设计在塑造高风险表格应用中稳定性-成本权衡方面的作用。

Abstract: Machine learning models deployed in non-stationary environments are exposed to temporal distribution shift, which can erode predictive reliability over time. While common mitigation strategies such as periodic retraining and recalibration aim to preserve performance, they typically focus on average metrics evaluated at isolated time points and do not explicitly model how reliability evolves during deployment.
  We propose a deployment-centric framework that treats reliability as a dynamic state composed of discrimination and calibration. The trajectory of this state across sequential evaluation windows induces a measurable notion of volatility, allowing deployment adaptation to be formulated as a multi-objective control problem that balances reliability stability against cumulative intervention cost.
  Within this framework, we define a family of state-dependent intervention policies and empirically characterize the resulting cost-volatility Pareto frontier. Experiments on a large-scale, temporally indexed credit-risk dataset (1.35M loans, 2007-2018) show that selective, drift-triggered interventions can achieve smoother reliability trajectories than continuous rolling retraining while substantially reducing operational cost.
  These findings position deployment reliability under temporal shift as a controllable multi-objective system and highlight the role of policy design in shaping stability-cost trade-offs in high-stakes tabular applications.

</details>


### [99] [An Initial Exploration of Contrastive Prompt Tuning to Generate Energy-Efficient Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02352)
*Sophie Weidmann,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出对比提示调优（CPT）方法，结合对比学习和提示调优技术，旨在优化大语言模型生成更节能的代码，以支持绿色软件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能够生成功能正确的代码，但与人工编写的解决方案相比，它们往往产生能效较低的代码。这种低效性导致更高的计算开销，与旨在减少代码能耗的绿色软件开发（GSD）目标直接冲突。因此需要研究如何优化LLM以促进生成节能代码。

Method: 采用对比提示调优（CPT）方法，该方法结合了对比学习技术（帮助模型区分高效和低效代码）和提示调优（一种参数高效微调方法，成本仅为传统微调的一小部分）。研究在Python、Java和C++编码问题上对三种不同模型进行了全面评估。

Result: CPT方法在两个模型上实现了代码准确性的持续改进，但能效提升因模型、编程语言和任务复杂性而异。这表明改进效果并非在所有情况下都可靠，能效增益存在变化性。

Conclusion: 对比提示调优能够在一定程度上优化大语言模型生成更节能的代码，但效果受多种因素影响，包括模型类型、编程语言和任务复杂性，表明需要更精细化的方法来确保能效改进的可靠性。

Abstract: Although LLMs are capable of generating functionally correct code, they also tend to produce less energy-efficient code in comparison to human-written solutions. As these inefficiencies lead to higher computational overhead, they are in direct conflict with Green Software Development (GSD) efforts, which aim to reduce the energy consumption of code. To support these efforts, this study aims to investigate whether and how LLMs can be optimized to promote the generation of energy-efficient code. To this end, we employ Contrastive Prompt Tuning (CPT). CPT combines Contrastive Learning techniques, which help the model to distinguish between efficient and inefficient code, and Prompt Tuning, a Parameter-Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) approach that requires only a fraction of the cost of traditional fine tuning. This study evaluates CPT on Python, Java and C++ coding problems across three different models to provide a comprehensive evaluation. The method achieves consistent improvements in code accuracy for two models but efficiency gains vary by model, language and task complexity, indicating that improvements are not uniformly reliable.

</details>


### [100] [From Broad Exploration to Stable Synthesis: Entropy-Guided Optimization for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02355)
*Han Song,Yucheng Zhou,Jianbing Shen,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过熵分析揭示了CoT与RL在文本到图像生成中的相互作用机制，并提出了一种基于熵引导的优化策略EG-GRPO，在标准基准测试中取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然结合思维链（CoT）和强化学习（RL）能改善文本到图像（T2I）生成，但CoT的探索机制与RL的优化过程之间的相互作用尚不明确。需要系统分析这种交互作用，以更好地理解并改进T2I生成方法。

Method: 提出基于熵的系统性分析框架，揭示了三个关键发现：1) CoT扩展生成探索空间，RL将其收缩至高奖励区域；2) 最终奖励与图像标记熵的均值和方差呈强负相关；3) 文本CoT的熵直接控制下游图像质量。基于这些发现，提出了熵引导的组相对策略优化（EG-GRPO），根据不确定性重新分配优化预算：低熵标记被排除在奖励驱动更新外以保持稳定性，高熵标记获得熵奖励以鼓励结构化探索。

Result: 在标准T2I基准测试中，EG-GRPO实现了最先进的性能。熵分析表明降低不确定性（熵）和减少不稳定性（方差）对提高生成质量至关重要，低熵CoT能产生更好的生成结果。

Conclusion: 通过熵分析揭示了CoT与RL在T2I生成中的相互作用机制，证明了控制生成过程中的不确定性对提高质量的重要性。提出的EG-GRPO策略通过基于熵的优化预算分配，在保持稳定性的同时鼓励结构化探索，为T2I生成提供了有效的优化方法。

Abstract: Combining Chain-of-Thought (CoT) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) improves text-to-image (T2I) generation, yet the underlying interaction between CoT's exploration and RL's optimization remains unclear. We present a systematic entropy-based analysis that yields three key insights: (1) CoT expands the generative exploration space, while RL contracts it toward high-reward regions; (2) final reward is strongly negatively correlated with both the mean and variance of image-token entropy, highlighting the need to reduce uncertainty and instability; and (3) the entropy of the textual CoT directly governs downstream image quality, with lower-entropy CoTs leading to better generations. Motivated by these findings, we propose Entropy-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization (EG-GRPO), a fine-tuning strategy that reallocates optimization budget by uncertainty: low-entropy tokens are excluded from reward-driven updates to preserve stability, while high-entropy tokens receive an entropy bonus that encourages structured exploration without collapse. Experiments on standard T2I benchmarks demonstrate that EG-GRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [101] [Dynamical structure of vanishing gradient and overfitting in multi-layer perceptrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02393)
*Alex Alì Maleknia,Yuzuru Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入最小模型，从动力学角度分析多层感知机训练中的梯度消失和过拟合问题，证明在有限噪声数据集上，MLP必然收敛到过拟合解而非理论最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度消失和过拟合是机器学习中广泛研究的问题，但现有研究多在渐近设定下进行，掩盖了导致这些问题出现的底层动力学机制。本文旨在为多层感知机的学习过程提供清晰的动力学描述。

Method: 引入受Fukumizu和Amari研究启发的简化模型，分析梯度下降训练MLP时的梯度消失和过拟合现象。该模型能够捕捉学习动力学中的关键特征，包括平台区域和近最优区域。

Result: 研究表明学习动力学会经过由鞍点结构组成的平台区域和近最优区域，最终收敛到过拟合区域。在训练数据集满足适当条件下，过拟合区域以高概率坍缩为单一吸引子（模对称性）。任何在有限噪声数据集上训练的MLP都无法收敛到理论最优解，必然收敛到过拟合解。

Conclusion: 从动力学视角揭示了梯度消失和过拟合的内在机制，证明了在有限噪声数据集上，过拟合是MLP训练的必然结果，而非可避免的异常现象。这为理解深度学习训练动态提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Vanishing gradient and overfitting are two of the most extensively studied problems in the literature about machine learning. However, they are frequently considered in some asymptotic setting, which obscure the underlying dynamical mechanisms responsible for their emergence. In this paper, we aim to provide a clear dynamical description of learning in multi-layer perceptrons. To this end, we introduce a minimal model, inspired by studies by Fukumizu and Amari, to investigate vanishing gradients and overfitting in MLPs trained via gradient descent. Within this model, we show that the learning dynamics may pass through plateau regions and near-optimal regions during training, both of which consist of saddle structures, before ultimately converging to the overfitting region. Under suitable conditions on the training dataset, we prove that, with high probability, the overfitting region collapses to a single attractor modulo symmetry, which corresponds to the overfitting. Moreover, we show that any MLP trained on a finite noisy dataset cannot converge to the theoretical optimum and instead necessarily converges to an overfitting solution.

</details>


### [102] [Conditional Sampling via Wasserstein Autoencoders and Triangular Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02644)
*Mohammad Al-Jarrah,Michele Martino,Marcus Yim,Bamdad Hosseini,Amirhossein Taghvaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件Wasserstein自编码器(CWAEs)，利用条件变量和被条件变量的低维结构进行条件模拟，相比低秩集合卡尔曼滤波器(LREnKF)显著降低近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件模拟方法未能充分利用条件变量和被条件变量的低维结构，导致在高维问题中效率低下。需要开发能够同时利用两者低维结构的新框架。

Method: 修改Wasserstein自编码器，使用(块)三角解码器并对潜变量施加适当的独立性假设。提出三种架构变体，建立与条件最优传输问题的理论联系。

Result: CWAEs在条件测度支撑真正低维的问题中，相比低秩集合卡尔曼滤波器(LREnKF)实现近似误差的显著降低。不同变体在不同场景下表现良好。

Conclusion: CWAEs为条件模拟提供了有效框架，能够同时利用条件变量和被条件变量的低维结构，在条件测度支撑低维的问题中表现优异，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: We present Conditional Wasserstein Autoencoders (CWAEs), a framework for conditional simulation that exploits low-dimensional structure in both the conditioned and the conditioning variables. The key idea is to modify a Wasserstein autoencoder to use a (block-) triangular decoder and impose an appropriate independence assumption on the latent variables. We show that the resulting model gives an autoencoder that can exploit low-dimensional structure while simultaneously the decoder can be used for conditional simulation. We explore various theoretical properties of CWAEs, including their connections to conditional optimal transport (OT) problems. We also present alternative formulations that lead to three architectural variants forming the foundation of our algorithms. We present a series of numerical experiments that demonstrate that our different CWAE variants achieve substantial reductions in approximation error relative to the low-rank ensemble Kalman filter (LREnKF), particularly in problems where the support of the conditional measures is truly low-dimensional.

</details>


### [103] [Mitigating Data Scarcity in Spaceflight Applications for Offline Reinforcement Learning Using Physics-Informed Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02438)
*Alex E. Ballentine,Nachiket U. Bapat,Raghvendra V. Cowlagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MI-VAE方法，通过物理信息生成模型解决空间飞行中RL控制器的sim-to-real差距和数据稀缺问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL控制器在物理系统部署时存在sim-to-real泛化差距，尤其在空间飞行领域，由于成本高昂和行星探索数据有限，真实世界训练数据稀缺。传统方法如系统识别和合成数据生成依赖充足数据且常因建模假设或缺乏物理约束而失败。

Method: 提出基于互信息的分割变分自编码器（MI-VAE），这是一种物理信息VAE，学习观测系统轨迹与物理模型预测轨迹之间的差异。MI-VAE的潜在空间能够生成尊重物理约束的合成数据集。

Result: 在行星着陆器问题上评估MI-VAE，关注有限真实数据和离线RL训练。结果显示，用MI-VAE样本增强数据集显著提高了下游RL性能，在统计保真度、样本多样性和策略成功率方面优于标准VAE。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了一种可扩展的策略，用于增强复杂、数据受限环境中自主控制器的鲁棒性，为解决sim-to-real差距和数据稀缺问题提供了新方法。

Abstract: The deployment of reinforcement learning (RL)-based controllers on physical systems is often limited by poor generalization to real-world scenarios, known as the simulation-to-reality (sim-to-real) gap. This gap is particularly challenging in spaceflight, where real-world training data are scarce due to high cost and limited planetary exploration data. Traditional approaches, such as system identification and synthetic data generation, depend on sufficient data and often fail due to modeling assumptions or lack of physics-based constraints. We propose addressing this data scarcity by introducing physics-based learning bias in a generative model. Specifically, we develop the Mutual Information-based Split Variational Autoencoder (MI-VAE), a physics-informed VAE that learns differences between observed system trajectories and those predicted by physics-based models. The latent space of the MI-VAE enables generation of synthetic datasets that respect physical constraints. We evaluate MI-VAE on a planetary lander problem, focusing on limited real-world data and offline RL training. Results show that augmenting datasets with MI-VAE samples significantly improves downstream RL performance, outperforming standard VAEs in statistical fidelity, sample diversity, and policy success rate. This work demonstrates a scalable strategy for enhancing autonomous controller robustness in complex, data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [104] [Matrix Profile for Time-Series Anomaly Detection: A Reproducible Open-Source Benchmark on TSB-AD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02445)
*Chin-Chia Michael Yeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MMPAD是一个基于矩阵轮廓的时间序列异常检测开源系统，针对TSB-AD基准测试设计，结合了多维聚合、高效kNN检索和移动平均后处理技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵轮廓方法虽然具有可解释性和可扩展性，但在时间序列异常检测中，要获得强基准性能仍需超越简单的最近邻轮廓设计。需要为TSB-AD基准测试提供一个可复现的矩阵轮廓方法参考实现。

Method: 系统结合了预排序的多维聚合、支持排除区的高效k最近邻检索（用于重复异常检测）以及移动平均后处理。提供了单变量和多变量时间序列的超参数设置。

Result: 提供了在TSB-AD基准测试上的详细结果，包括在总体排行榜上的表现以及针对特定数据集特征的分析。开源实现已发布在GitHub上。

Conclusion: MMPAD为基于矩阵轮廓的时间序列异常检测在TSB-AD基准测试上提供了一个可复现的参考实现，展示了如何通过系统设计选择提升性能，并提供了详细的实现细节和结果分析。

Abstract: Matrix Profile (MP) methods are an interpretable and scalable family of distance-based methods for time-series anomaly detection, but strong benchmark performance still depends on design choices beyond a vanilla nearest-neighbor profile. This technical report documents an open-source Matrix Profile for Anomaly Detection (MMPAD) submission to TSB-AD, a benchmark that covers both univariate and multivariate time series. The submitted system combines pre-sorted multidimensional aggregation, efficient exclusion-zone-aware k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) retrieval for repeated anomalies, and moving-average post-processing. To serve as a reproducible reference for MP-based anomaly detection on TSB-AD, we detail the released implementation, the hyperparameter settings for the univariate and multivariate tracks, and the corresponding benchmark results. We further analyze how the system performs on the aggregate leaderboard and across specific dataset characteristics.The open-source implementation is available at https://github.com/mcyeh/mmpad_tsb.

</details>


### [105] [Do We Need Frontier Models to Verify Mathematical Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02450)
*Aaditya Naik,Guruprerana Shabadi,Rajeev Alur,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM在数学证明验证任务中的表现，发现小模型在准确性上仅落后前沿模型约10%，但自一致性差25%，通过提示工程可显著提升小模型性能至前沿模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿推理模型在数学竞赛和开放问题中取得突破，验证其自然语言证明的正确性变得至关重要。LLM作为验证工具的需求日益增长，但可靠验证需要何种模型能力尚不明确。本研究旨在系统评估不同LLM在竞赛级数学证明验证任务中的表现，探索小模型与前沿模型之间的能力差距。

Method: 研究系统评估了4个开源模型和2个前沿LLM在人类评分的竞赛级数学证明数据集上。采用两个关键指标：验证器准确性和自一致性（同一证明重复判断的一致性）。通过LLM引导的提示搜索，合成专门化提示集合来克服小模型的特定失败模式。

Result: 小模型在准确性上仅落后前沿模型约10%，但自一致性差25%。所有模型的验证准确性都对提示选择敏感。小模型实际上具备与前沿模型相当的数学验证能力，但难以通过通用判断提示可靠激发这些能力。通过专门化提示集合，小模型的准确性提升达9.1%，自一致性提升15.9%，使Qwen3.5-35B等模型在证明验证任务上达到与Gemini 3.1 Pro相当的水平。

Conclusion: 可靠的自然语言证明验证不仅需要数学推理能力，更需要一致的判断能力。小模型在数学能力上接近前沿模型，但自一致性较差。通过针对性的提示工程可以显著提升小模型的验证性能，使其达到前沿模型水平，这为高效利用较小模型进行证明验证提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Advances in training, post-training, and inference-time methods have enabled frontier reasoning models to win gold medals in math competitions and settle challenging open problems. Gaining trust in the responses of these models requires that natural language proofs be checked for errors. LLM judges are increasingly being adopted to meet the growing demand for evaluating such proofs. While verification is considered easier than generation, what model capability does reliable verification actually require? We systematically evaluate four open-source and two frontier LLMs on datasets of human-graded natural language proofs of competition-level problems. We consider two key metrics: verifier accuracy and self-consistency (the rate of agreement across repeated judgments on the same proof). We observe that smaller open-source models are only up to ~10% behind frontier models in accuracy but they are up to ~25% more inconsistent. Furthermore, we see that verifier accuracy is sensitive to prompt choice across all models. We then demonstrate that the smaller models, in fact, do possess the mathematical capabilities to verify proofs at the level of frontier models, but they struggle to reliably elicit these capabilities with general judging prompts. Through an LLM-guided prompt search, we synthesize an ensemble of specialized prompts that overcome the specific failure modes of smaller models, boosting their performance by up to 9.1% in accuracy and 15.9% in self-consistency. These gains are realized across models and datasets, allowing models like Qwen3.5-35B to perform on par with frontier models such as Gemini 3.1 Pro for proof verification.

</details>


### [106] [On the Geometric Structure of Layer Updates in Deep Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02459)
*Jun-Sik Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现深度语言模型的层更新具有几何结构分解：主导的tokenwise分量和几何上不同的残差分量，后者承载重要功能计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究深度语言模型中层更新的几何结构，而非中间表示编码的信息，探索表示如何从一层到另一层变化。

Method: 提出架构无关的框架，将层更新分解为主导tokenwise分量和残差分量，分析几何对齐、角度偏差和投影特性。

Result: 层更新几乎完美对齐tokenwise分量，残差分量几何上不同；受限tokenwise模型的近似误差与输出扰动强相关（Spearman相关常超0.7，大模型中可达0.95）。

Conclusion: 大多数层更新表现为沿主导方向的结构化重参数化，而功能上重要的计算集中在几何上不同的残差分量中。

Abstract: We study the geometric structure of layer updates in deep language models. Rather than analyzing what information is encoded in intermediate representations, we ask how representations change from one layer to the next. We show that layerwise updates admit a decomposition into a dominant tokenwise component and a residual that is not captured by restricted tokenwise function classes.
  Across multiple architectures, including Transformers and state-space models, we find that the full layer update is almost perfectly aligned with the tokenwise component, while the residual exhibits substantially weaker alignment, larger angular deviation, and significantly lower projection onto the dominant tokenwise subspace. This indicates that the residual is not merely a small correction, but a geometrically distinct component of the transformation.
  This geometric separation has functional consequences: approximation error under the restricted tokenwise model is strongly associated with output perturbation, with Spearman correlations often exceeding 0.7 and reaching up to 0.95 in larger models. Together, these results suggest that most layerwise updates behave like structured reparameterizations along a dominant direction, while functionally significant computation is concentrated in a geometrically distinct residual component.
  Our framework provides a simple, architecture-agnostic method for probing the geometric and functional structure of layer updates in modern language models.

</details>


### [107] [Time-Warping Recurrent Neural Networks for Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02474)
*Jonathon Hirschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于时间扭曲的RNN迁移学习方法，通过时间缩放将预训练模型应用于不同时间尺度的物理系统，并在野火燃料湿度预测中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统在不同环境条件下演化速度不同，传统RNN模型难以适应不同时间尺度的系统。需要一种高效的迁移学习方法，使预训练模型能够适应不同时间尺度的物理过程，同时保持预测精度。

Method: 提出基于时间扭曲的RNN迁移学习方法：1) 证明LSTM能够以任意精度逼近线性一阶时滞微分方程系统；2) 通过时间缩放（时间扭曲）调整模型时间尺度；3) 在野火燃料湿度预测中应用该方法，将预训练于10小时时间尺度的LSTM模型迁移到1小时、100小时和1000小时时间尺度的燃料预测。

Result: 时间扭曲方法在燃料湿度预测任务中表现与现有迁移学习方法相当，但仅修改了很少的参数（其他方法需要修改大量参数）。该方法成功将10小时时间尺度的预训练模型迁移到不同时间尺度的燃料预测中，保持了预测精度。

Conclusion: 时间扭曲是一种有效的RNN迁移学习方法，特别适用于物理系统的时间尺度变化问题。该方法通过最小化参数修改实现了模型适应，为不同时间尺度物理过程的建模提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamical systems describe how a physical system evolves over time. Physical processes can evolve faster or slower in different environmental conditions. We use time-warping as rescaling the time in a model of a physical system. This thesis proposes a new method of transfer learning for Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) based on time-warping. We prove that for a class of linear, first-order differential equations known as time lag models, an LSTM can approximate these systems with any desired accuracy, and the model can be time-warped while maintaining the approximation accuracy.
  The Time-Warping method of transfer learning is then evaluated in an applied problem on predicting fuel moisture content (FMC), an important concept in wildfire modeling. An RNN with LSTM recurrent layers is pretrained on fuels with a characteristic time scale of 10 hours, where there are large quantities of data available for training. The RNN is then modified with transfer learning to generate predictions for fuels with characteristic time scales of 1 hour, 100 hours, and 1000 hours. The Time-Warping method is evaluated against several known methods of transfer learning. The Time-Warping method produces predictions with an accuracy level comparable to the established methods, despite modifying only a small fraction of the parameters that the other methods modify.

</details>


### [108] [SEDGE: Structural Extrapolated Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02482)
*Kun Zhang,Jiaqi Sun,Yiqing Li,Ignavier Ng,Namrata Deka,Shaoan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEDGE框架：基于数据生成过程的结构假设，实现可靠的外推数据生成，包括理论条件和实用算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在数据生成过程中，如何基于现有数据结构和假设，可靠地生成满足新规范的外推数据的问题

Method: 提出结构外推数据生成框架，基于结构信息优化策略或扩散后验采样两种实用方法实现外推数据生成

Result: 在合成数据上验证了外推性能，并在外推图像生成等实际场景中证明了框架的有效性

Conclusion: SEDGE框架为结构外推数据生成提供了理论基础和实用算法，能够可靠地生成满足新规范的数据

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for Structural Extrapolated Data GEneration (SEDGE) based on suitable assumptions on the underlying data generating process. We provide conditions under which data satisfying new specifications can be generated reliably, together with the approximate identifiability of the distribution of such data under certain ``conservative" assumptions. On the algorithmic side, we develop practical methods to achieve extrapolated data generation, based on the structure-informed optimization strategy or diffusion posterior sampling, respectively. We verify the extrapolation performance on synthetic data and also consider extrapolated image generation as a real-world scenario to illustrate the validity of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [109] [Re-analysis of the Human Transcription Factor Atlas Recovers TF-Specific Signatures from Pooled Single-Cell Screens with Missing Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02511)
*Arka Jain,Umesh Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对TF Atlas单细胞扰动数据集进行重新分析，开发可重复流程恢复TF特异性信号，即使缺乏内部对照也能通过外部基准和背景减除获得有效结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共单细胞扰动图谱是研究转录因子功能的重要资源，但下游再分析常受限于不完整的元数据和缺失的内部对照，需要开发方法从现有数据中恢复可靠的TF特异性信号

Method: 重新分析人类TF Atlas数据集，建立可重复流程进行质量控制、MORF条形码解复用、每TF差异表达分析和功能富集；使用胚胎体细胞作为外部基准，通过背景减除去除共享的批次/转导伪影

Result: 从77,018个细胞中成功分配60,997个(79.2%)到87个TF身份；背景减除策略使59/61个可测试TF恢复特异性信号（vs. 仅27个通过one-vs-rest检测）；识别出HOPX、MAZ、PAX6、FOS、FEZF2为最强转录重塑因子；TF特异性富集分析揭示功能关联；与已发表排名显著一致(Spearman ρ=-0.316, p=0.013)

Conclusion: 即使缺乏内部对照，通过原则性外部对照、伪影去除和可重复计算，TF Atlas数据仍能支持经过验证的TF特异性转录和通路分析，为公共扰动数据集的再分析提供方法学框架

Abstract: Public pooled single-cell perturbation atlases are valuable resources for studying transcription factor (TF) function, but downstream re-analysis can be limited by incomplete deposited metadata and missing internal controls. Here we re-analyze the human TF Atlas dataset (GSE216481), a MORF-based pooled overexpression screen spanning 3,550 TF open reading frames and 254,519 cells, with a reproducible pipeline for quality control, MORF barcode demultiplexing, per-TF differential expression, and functional enrichment. From 77,018 cells in the pooled screen, we assign 60,997 (79.2\%) to 87 TF identities. Because the deposited barcode mapping lacks the GFP and mCherry negative controls present in the original library, we use embryoid body (EB) cells as an external baseline and remove shared batch/transduction artifacts by background subtraction. This strategy recovers TF-specific signatures for 59 of 61 testable TFs, compared with 27 detected by one-vs-rest alone, showing that robust TF-level signal can be rescued despite missing intra-pool controls. HOPX, MAZ, PAX6, FOS, and FEZF2 emerge as the strongest transcriptional remodelers, while per-TF enrichment links FEZF2 to regulation of differentiation, EGR1 to Hippo and cardiac programs, FOS to focal adhesion, and NFIC to collagen biosynthesis. Condition-level analyses reveal convergent Wnt, neurogenic, EMT, and Hippo signatures, and Harmony indicates minimal confounding batch effects across pooled replicates. Our per-TF effect sizes significantly agree with Joung et al.'s published rankings (Spearman $ρ= -0.316$, $p = 0.013$; negative because lower rank indicates stronger effect). Together, these results show that the deposited TF Atlas data can support validated TF-specific transcriptional and pathway analyses when paired with principled external controls, artifact removal, and reproducible computation.

</details>


### [110] [AdaHOP: Fast and Accurate Low-Precision Training via Outlier-Pattern-Aware Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02525)
*Seonggon Kim,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaHOP通过自适应Hadamard变换和异常值模式感知策略，在MXFP4精度下实现BF16训练质量，达到3.6倍内存压缩和1.8倍内核加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度训练方法采用固定Hadamard变换来抑制异常值，但忽略了不同张量间异常值结构的显著差异，导致量化误差抑制效果不佳

Method: 首先系统研究LLM权重、激活和梯度中的异常值模式，将其分为行向、列向和无异常值三类；然后提出AdaHOP，根据每个矩阵乘法的异常值模式自适应选择最优策略：内Hadamard变换或结合选择性异常值提取的IHT

Result: AdaHOP在MXFP4精度下达到BF16训练质量，实现3.6倍内存压缩和1.8倍内核加速，优于固定变换方法

Conclusion: 异常值模式在不同层和计算路径中存在显著差异，需要自适应处理策略；AdaHOP通过模式感知的自适应Hadamard变换和硬件优化内核，在极低精度下保持了训练质量并显著提升了效率

Abstract: Low-precision training (LPT) commonly employs Hadamard transforms to suppress outliers and mitigate quantization error in large language models (LLMs). However, prior methods apply a fixed transform uniformly, despite substantial variation in outlier structures across tensors. Through the first systematic study of outlier patterns across weights, activations, and gradients of LLMs, we show that this strategy is fundamentally flawed: the effectiveness of Hadamard-based suppression depends on how the transform's smoothing direction aligns with the outlier structure of each operand -- a property that varies substantially across layers and computation paths. We characterize these patterns into three types: Row-wise, Column-wise, and None. Each pair requires a tailored transform direction or outlier handling strategy to minimize quantization error. Based on this insight, we propose AdaHOP (Adaptive Hadamard transform with Outlier-Pattern-aware strategy), which assigns each matrix multiplication its optimal strategy: Inner Hadamard Transform (IHT) where inner-dimension smoothing is effective, or IHT combined with selective Outlier Extraction (OE) -- routing dominant outliers to a high-precision path -- where it is not. Combined with hardware-aware Triton kernels, AdaHOP achieves BF16 training quality at MXFP4 precision while delivering up to 3.6X memory compression and 1.8X kernel acceleration} over BF16 full-precision training.

</details>


### [111] [Jump Start or False Start? A Theoretical and Empirical Evaluation of LLM-initialized Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02527)
*Adam Bayley,Xiaodan Zhu,Raquel Aoki,Yanshuai Cao,Kevin H. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探讨了LLM生成偏好数据在随机噪声和系统错配下对上下文多臂老虎机预热启动的影响，发现噪声容忍度约30%，超过50%会损害性能，系统错配时LLM先验可能比冷启动更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设LLM生成的用户偏好数据与实际偏好合理对齐，但缺乏对噪声和系统错配影响的系统分析。需要探究LLM生成数据在噪声污染下的鲁棒性，以及何时LLM预热启动优于冷启动。

Method: 通过向LLM生成的合成训练数据注入随机噪声和标签翻转噪声，系统测试预热启动效果；开发理论分析框架，分解随机标签噪声和系统错配对先验误差的影响；推导LLM预热启动优于冷启动的充分条件；在多个联合分析数据集和LLM上进行验证。

Result: 对齐领域：预热启动在30%噪声下仍有效，40%左右失去优势，超过50%会损害性能；系统错配时，即使无额外噪声，LLM生成先验可能导致比冷启动更高的遗憾；理论分析解释了噪声和错配的影响机制；估计的对齐度能可靠预测预热启动何时改善或降低推荐质量。

Conclusion: LLM生成的偏好数据对随机噪声有一定鲁棒性，但系统错配可能使预热启动适得其反；提出的理论框架和充分条件为判断何时使用LLM预热启动提供了指导；对齐度估计可作为实用的性能预测指标。

Abstract: The recent advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) offers new opportunities to generate user preference data to warm-start bandits. Recent studies on contextual bandits with LLM initialization (CBLI) have shown that these synthetic priors can significantly lower early regret. However, these findings assume that LLM-generated choices are reasonably aligned with actual user preferences. In this paper, we systematically examine how LLM-generated preferences perform when random and label-flipping noise is injected into the synthetic training data. For aligned domains, we find that warm-starting remains effective up to 30% corruption, loses its advantage around 40%, and degrades performance beyond 50%. When there is systematic misalignment, even without added noise, LLM-generated priors can lead to higher regret than a cold-start bandit. To explain these behaviors, we develop a theoretical analysis that decomposes the effect of random label noise and systematic misalignment on the prior error driving the bandit's regret, and derive a sufficient condition under which LLM-based warm starts are provably better than a cold-start bandit. We validate these results across multiple conjoint datasets and LLMs, showing that estimated alignment reliably tracks when warm-starting improves or degrades recommendation quality.

</details>


### [112] [A Spectral Framework for Multi-Scale Nonlinear Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02535)
*Zeyang Huang,Angelos Chatzimparmpas,Thomas Höllt,Takanori Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于谱分解的非线性降维框架，通过谱基与交叉熵优化实现多尺度表示，解决全局-局部保留权衡和可解释性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统降维方法存在的两个长期权衡：1）全局-局部保留矛盾（如t-SNE/UMAP侧重局部但扭曲全局，Laplacian Eigenmaps保留全局但局部分离有限）；2）表达能力与解析透明度之间的差距（非线性方法缺乏与高维结构的显式连接）

Method: 采用谱框架结合谱基与交叉熵优化进行非线性降维，支持多尺度表示以桥接全局和局部结构。利用线性谱分解，通过图频率视角分析嵌入，研究谱模式对嵌入结果的影响，并辅以基于glyph的散点图增强进行可视化探索

Result: 定量评估和案例研究表明，该框架提高了流形连续性，同时通过谱模式贡献分析实现了对嵌入结构的深入分析

Conclusion: 提出的谱框架有效解决了降维中的全局-局部权衡问题，并通过谱分解提供了更好的嵌入过程可解释性，支持多尺度分析和可视化探索

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction (DR) is characterized by two longstanding trade-offs. First, there is a global-local preservation tension: methods such as t-SNE and UMAP prioritize local neighborhood preservation, yet may distort global manifold structure, while methods such as Laplacian Eigenmaps preserve global geometry but often yield limited local separation. Second, there is a gap between expressiveness and analytical transparency: many nonlinear DR methods produce embeddings without an explicit connection to the underlying high-dimensional structure, limiting insight into the embedding process. In this paper, we introduce a spectral framework for nonlinear DR that addresses these challenges. Our approach embeds high-dimensional data using a spectral basis combined with cross-entropy optimization, enabling multi-scale representations that bridge global and local structure. Leveraging linear spectral decomposition, the framework further supports analysis of embeddings through a graph-frequency perspective, enabling examination of how spectral modes influence the resulting embedding. We complement this analysis with glyph-based scatterplot augmentations for visual exploration. Quantitative evaluations and case studies demonstrate that our framework improves manifold continuity while enabling deeper analysis of embedding structure through spectral mode contributions.

</details>


### [113] [Fast NF4 Dequantization Kernels for Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02556)
*Xiangbo Qi,Chaoyi Jiang,Murali Annavaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级共享内存优化技术，解决NF4量化LLM推理时的性能瓶颈，相比BitsAndBytes实现获得2.0-2.2倍内核加速和最高1.54倍端到端提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型超出单GPU内存容量，需要量化技术。NF4量化实现4倍内存压缩，但在NVIDIA GPU上推理时需要昂贵的反量化到FP16格式，形成关键性能瓶颈。

Method: 通过原则性的内存层次利用，设计轻量级共享内存优化。利用共享内存相比全局内存12-15倍的延迟优势，简化索引逻辑减少指令数，每个线程块仅使用64字节共享内存。

Result: 在三个模型（Gemma 27B、Qwen3 32B、Llama3.3 70B）上实现2.0-2.2倍内核加速，最高1.54倍端到端改进。保持完全生态系统兼容性，提供即插即用解决方案。

Conclusion: 轻量级优化能以最小工程努力带来显著性能提升，为HuggingFace生态系统提供民主化访问先进模型的解决方案，可在现有GPU基础设施上部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have grown beyond the memory capacity of single GPU devices, necessitating quantization techniques for practical deployment. While NF4 (4-bit NormalFloat) quantization enables 4$\times$ memory reduction, inference on current NVIDIA GPUs (e.g., Ampere A100) requires expensive dequantization back to FP16 format, creating a critical performance bottleneck. This paper presents a lightweight shared memory optimization that addresses this gap through principled memory hierarchy exploitation while maintaining full ecosystem compatibility. We compare our technique against the open-source BitsAndBytes implementation, achieving 2.0--2.2$\times$ kernel speedup across three models (Gemma 27B, Qwen3 32B, and Llama3.3 70B) and up to 1.54$\times$ end-to-end improvement by leveraging the 12--15$\times$ latency advantage of shared memory over global memory access. Our optimization reduces instruction counts through simplified indexing logic while using only 64 bytes of shared memory per thread block, demonstrating that lightweight optimizations can deliver substantial performance gains with minimal engineering effort. This work provides a plug-and-play solution for the HuggingFace ecosystem that democratizes access to advanced models on existing GPU infrastructure.

</details>


### [114] [ROMAN: A Multiscale Routing Operator for Convolutional Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02577)
*Gonzalo Uribarri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ROMAN是一种确定性时间序列算子，通过构建抗混叠多尺度金字塔、提取固定长度窗口并堆叠为伪通道，将时间尺度和粗粒度时间位置映射到显式通道结构中，同时减少序列长度，为卷积分类器提供紧凑表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准卷积分类器在处理时间序列时通常通过池化操作减少时间维度，这可能导致丢失重要的时间结构信息（如粗粒度位置、长程相关性、多尺度交互等）。需要一种机制能够控制下游模型的归纳偏置，同时提高计算效率。

Method: ROMAN算子构建抗混叠多尺度金字塔，从每个尺度提取固定长度窗口，将这些窗口堆叠为伪通道，形成紧凑表示。该方法将时间尺度和粗粒度时间位置编码到通道结构中，减少序列长度，使标准卷积分类器能够操作。

Result: 在合成任务中，ROMAN在粗粒度位置感知、长程相关性、多尺度交互和完全位置不变性方面表现符合预期机制，在标准池化卷积倾向于抑制的时间结构信息上最有效。在UCR和UEA档案的长序列子集上，ROMAN的效果因任务而异，但计算效率通常有优势。

Conclusion: ROMAN提供了一种简单机制来控制下游模型的归纳偏置：减少时间不变性、使时间池化隐含粗粒度位置感知、通过通道混合暴露多尺度交互，同时通过缩短处理的时间轴提高计算效率，为时间序列分类提供了实用的替代表示方法。

Abstract: We introduce ROMAN (ROuting Multiscale representAtioN), a deterministic operator for time series that maps temporal scale and coarse temporal position into an explicit channel structure while reducing sequence length. ROMAN builds an anti-aliased multiscale pyramid, extracts fixed-length windows from each scale, and stacks them as pseudochannels, yielding a compact representation on which standard convolutional classifiers can operate. In this way, ROMAN provides a simple mechanism to control the inductive bias of downstream models: it can reduce temporal invariance, make temporal pooling implicitly coarse-position-aware, and expose multiscale interactions through channel mixing, while often improving computational efficiency by shortening the processed time axis. We formally analyze the ROMAN operator and then evaluate it in two complementary ways by measuring its impact as a preprocessing step for four representative convolutional classifiers: MiniRocket, MultiRocket, a standard CNN-based classifier, and a fully convolutional network (FCN) classifier. First, we design synthetic time series classification tasks that isolate coarse position awareness, long-range correlation, multiscale interaction, and full positional invariance, showing that ROMAN behaves consistently with its intended mechanism and is most useful when class information depends on temporal structure that standard pooled convolution tends to suppress. Second, we benchmark the same models with and without ROMAN on long-sequence subsets of the UCR and UEA archives, showing that ROMAN provides a practically useful alternative representation whose effect on accuracy is task-dependent, but whose effect on efficiency is often favorable. Code is available at https://github.com/gon-uri/ROMAN

</details>


### [115] [VoxelCodeBench: Benchmarking 3D World Modeling Through Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02580)
*Yan Zheng,Florian Bordes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VoxelCode平台用于评估3D空间推理的代码生成模型，通过Unreal Engine执行代码并创建VoxelCodeBench基准测试，发现生成可执行代码比生成空间正确输出更容易


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估3D空间推理的代码生成模型需要在实际环境中执行生成的代码，并评估超越表面正确性的输出。现有方法缺乏统一的评估平台来测试模型在3D理解和环境创建方面的能力。

Method: 开发VoxelCode平台，集成自然语言任务规范、Unreal Engine中的API驱动代码执行，以及支持自动指标和人工评估的统一评估流水线。创建VoxelCodeBench基准测试，包含体素操作任务，涵盖三个推理维度：符号解释、几何构造和艺术组合。

Result: 评估领先的代码生成模型发现，生成可执行代码比生成空间正确输出更容易，几何构造和多对象组合特别具有挑战性。平台和基准测试已开源，为社区提供可扩展的基础设施。

Conclusion: VoxelCode平台和VoxelCodeBench基准测试为评估3D代码生成模型提供了有效工具，揭示了当前模型在空间推理方面的局限性，特别是几何构造和多对象组合任务。开源基础设施将促进未来模型在3D空间推理能力的发展。

Abstract: Evaluating code generation models for 3D spatial reasoning requires executing generated code in realistic environments and assessing outputs beyond surface-level correctness. We introduce a platform VoxelCode, for analyzing code generation capabilities for 3D understanding and environment creation. Our platform integrates natural language task specification, API-driven code execution in Unreal Engine, and a unified evaluation pipeline supporting both automated metrics and human assessment. To demonstrate its utility, we construct VoxelCodeBench, a benchmark of voxel manipulation tasks spanning three reasoning dimensions: symbolic interpretation, geometric construction, and artistic composition. Evaluating leading code generation models, we find that producing executable code is far easier than producing spatially correct outputs, with geometric construction and multi-object composition proving particularly challenging. By open-sourcing our platform and benchmark, we provide the community with extensible infrastructure for developing new 3D code generation benchmarks and probing spatial reasoning in future models.

</details>


### [116] [Steerable but Not Decodable: Function Vectors Operate Beyond the Logit Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02608)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 函数向量（FVs）在无法通过logit lens解码正确答案的情况下仍能成功引导LLM行为，表明FVs编码的是计算指令而非答案方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数向量引导失败的原因，最初假设是由于任务相关信息缺失导致logit lens和引导同时失败，但实际发现相反现象

Method: 进行跨模板FV转移研究：4,032对任务组合，12个任务，6个模型（来自3个家族），每个任务8个模板。分析FV词汇投影、最佳干预层、logit lens检测层，并进行激活修补验证

Result: 发现普遍存在"可引导但不可解码"模式：FV引导准确率始终超过logit lens，最大差距达-0.91。FV在早期层（L2-L8）干预最优，而logit lens仅在晚期层（L28-L32）检测到正确答案。模型家族间存在差异：Mistral FVs重写中间表示，Llama/Gemma FVs几乎不改变表示但引导成功

Conclusion: 函数向量编码的是计算指令而非答案方向，其引导机制与logit lens的解码能力解耦。先前报告的负余弦转移相关性在大规模研究中消失，表明任务身份比余弦相似性更重要

Abstract: Function vectors (FVs) -- mean-difference directions extracted from in-context learning demonstrations -- can steer large language model behavior when added to the residual stream. We hypothesized that FV steering failures reflect an absence of task-relevant information: the logit lens would fail alongside steering. We were wrong. In the most comprehensive cross-template FV transfer study to date - 4,032 pairs across 12 tasks, 6 models from 3 families (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B, Mistral-7B-v0.3; base and instruction-tuned), 8 templates per task - we find the opposite dissociation: FV steering succeeds even when the logit lens cannot decode the correct answer at any layer. This steerability-without-decodability pattern is universal: steering exceeds logit lens accuracy for every task on every model, with gaps as large as -0.91. Only 3 of 72 task-model instances show the predicted decodable-without-steerable pattern, all in Mistral. FV vocabulary projection reveals that FVs achieving over 0.90 steering accuracy still project to incoherent token distributions, indicating FVs encode computational instructions rather than answer directions. FVs intervene optimally at early layers (L2-L8); the logit lens detects correct answers only at late layers (L28-L32). The previously reported negative cosine-transfer correlation (r=-0.572) dissolves at scale: pooled r ranges from -0.199 to +0.126, and cosine adds less than 0.011 in R-squared beyond task identity. Post-steering analysis reveals a model-family divergence: Mistral FVs rewrite intermediate representations; Llama/Gemma FVs produce near-zero changes despite successful steering. Activation patching confirms causal localization: easy tasks achieve perfect recovery at targeted layers; hard tasks show zero recovery everywhere.

</details>


### [117] [Analytic Drift Resister for Non-Exemplar Continual Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02633)
*Lei Song,Shihan Guan,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADR是一个新颖的非示例持续图学习框架，通过分层分析合并和解析分类器重构，在避免隐私风险的同时有效抵抗特征漂移，实现零遗忘的类增量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非示例持续图学习(NECGL)方法通过保留类级原型而非原始图样本来避免隐私风险，但会导致特征漂移；而解析持续学习(ACL)方法利用冻结预训练模型增强持续学习性能，但会显著降低模型可塑性。需要解决这两个挑战。

Method: 提出解析漂移抵抗器(ADR)框架：1) 使用迭代反向传播打破冻结预训练约束，适应任务图分布变化并增强模型可塑性；2) 提出分层分析合并(HAM)，通过岭回归对GNN中的线性变换进行层间合并，绝对抵抗特征漂移；3) 解析分类器重构(ACR)实现理论上零遗忘的类增量学习。

Result: 在四个节点分类基准测试上的实证评估表明，ADR与现有最先进方法相比保持了强大的竞争力。

Conclusion: ADR是一个理论上有基础的非示例持续图学习框架，通过结合迭代反向传播、分层分析合并和解析分类器重构，有效解决了特征漂移和模型可塑性衰减的问题，在保护隐私的同时实现了高性能的持续学习。

Abstract: Non-Exemplar Continual Graph Learning (NECGL) seeks to eliminate the privacy risks intrinsic to rehearsal-based paradigms by retaining solely class-level prototype representations rather than raw graph examples for mitigating catastrophic forgetting. However, this design choice inevitably precipitates feature drift. As a nascent alternative, Analytic Continual Learning (ACL) capitalizes on the intrinsic generalization properties of frozen pre-trained models to bolster continual learning performance. Nonetheless, a key drawback resides in the pronounced attenuation of model plasticity. To surmount these challenges, we propose Analytic Drift Resister (ADR), a novel and theoretically grounded NECGL framework. ADR exploits iterative backpropagation to break free from the frozen pre-trained constraint, adapting to evolving task graph distributions and fortifying model plasticity. Since parameter updates trigger feature drift, we further propose Hierarchical Analytic Merging (HAM), performing layer-wise merging of linear transformations in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) via ridge regression, thereby ensuring absolute resistance to feature drift. On this basis, Analytic Classifier Reconstruction (ACR) enables theoretically zero-forgetting class-incremental learning. Empirical evaluation on four node classification benchmarks demonstrates that ADR maintains strong competitiveness against existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [118] [AXELRAM: Quantize Once, Never Dequantize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02638)
*Yasushi Nishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AXELRAM是一种智能SRAM宏架构，通过设计时固定码本直接计算量化KV缓存索引的注意力分数，无需反量化。采用正交变换量化将坐标分布集中到N(0,1/d)，使最优量化器仅依赖于维度d和位宽b，而非输入数据。非对称路径设计（写入时变换，读取时查表）将每查询乘法减少102.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少KV缓存注意力计算的硬件开销，避免反量化操作，通过固定码本实现高效计算。同时解决量化过程中出现的符号模式敏感性导致的灾难性性能下降问题。

Method: 1. 设计时固定码本：基于正交变换的量化将每个坐标分布集中到N(0,1/d)，使最优量化器仅依赖于维度d和位宽b。2. 非对称路径设计：写入时进行变换，读取时通过查表计算注意力分数，无需逆变换。3. 多种子评估发现符号模式敏感性问题，提出基于层间范数异质性的梯度无关符号模式选择方法（200个候选，8个校准样本，一次性）。

Result: 1. 每查询乘法操作减少102.4倍。2. 发现符号模式敏感性导致某些模型（如Qwen2.5-3B）出现灾难性PPL峰值（Δ>50），而其他模型（如LLaMA-3.1-8B）完全稳定。3. 提出的梯度无关符号模式选择方法消除了灾难性峰值，无需额外硬件成本。

Conclusion: AXELRAM通过固定码本和非对称路径设计实现了高效的KV缓存注意力计算，显著减少乘法操作。同时揭示了KV缓存量化中的符号模式敏感性现象，并提出有效的解决方案，为量化KV缓存的高效硬件实现提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose AXELRAM, a smart SRAM macro architecture that computes attention scores directly from quantized KV cache indices without dequantization. The key enabler is a design-time fixed codebook: orthogonal-transform-based quantization concentrates each coordinate's distribution to N(0,1/d), so the optimal quantizer depends only on dimension d and bit-width b, not on input data. The asymmetric path design -- transform on write, table-lookup on read with no inverse transform -- reduces per-query multiplications by 102.4x (a mathematical identity).
  Through multi-seed evaluation (10 seeds x 3 models), we discover that sign pattern sensitivity causes catastrophic PPL spikes (Delta > 50) on certain models (Qwen2.5-3B), while others (LLaMA-3.1-8B) are fully stable. This phenomenon extends SpinQuant's observation of rotation variance in weight quantization to the KV cache domain, where the effect is qualitatively more severe. We trace the root cause to layer-wise norm heterogeneity and propose a gradient-free sign pattern selection (200 candidates, 8 calibration samples, one-time) that eliminates catastrophic spikes with zero additional hardware cost. All source code is available at https://github.com/Axelidea/AXELRAM.

</details>


### [119] [Generalization Limits of Reinforcement Learning Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02652)
*Haruhi Shida,Koo Imai,Keigo Kansa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对OpenAI GPT-OSS-20B的复合越狱攻击，通过组合多种已被单独防御的攻击技术，使攻击成功率从14.3%提升至71.4%，证明安全训练泛化能力弱于模型能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论分析表明强化学习训练仅重新分配现有能力的利用概率，而非获得新能力。本研究旨在验证安全训练泛化能力不如模型能力这一假设，通过设计复合越狱攻击来探索对齐机制的泛化失败

Method: 提出"复合越狱"攻击方法，针对OpenAI GPT-OSS-20B模型，组合多种已被单独防御的攻击技术，通过饱和指令层次维护过程来利用对齐的泛化失败

Result: 攻击成功率从单独方法的14.3%显著提升至复合方法的71.4%，为安全训练泛化能力弱于模型能力的假设提供了实证证据

Conclusion: 安全训练泛化能力不如模型能力，需要采用复合攻击场景进行多方面的安全评估，单一防御措施不足以应对组合攻击

Abstract: The safety of large language models (LLMs) relies on alignment techniques such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). However, recent theoretical analyses suggest that reinforcement learning-based training does not acquire new capabilities but merely redistributes the utilization probabilities of existing ones. In this study, we propose ``compound jailbreaks'' targeting OpenAI gpt-oss-20b, which exploit the generalization failures of alignment. This approach combines multiple attack techniques -- each individually defended against -- to saturate the instruction hierarchy maintenance process.
